HOW TO USE THIS DOCUMENT
------------------------
Every block has its file name, ORIGINAL file line range, actual numbered code,
and a plain-language explanation. Numbers restart at 1 for each source file;
they are not the line positions in this text document. No source lines are
omitted from the reproduced files. A block may include surrounding blank lines
or a section comment to keep the original source sequence intact.

Use Find (Ctrl+F) for a topic such as "Customer identification", "Validate a
customer name", "Global menu escape", or a file name. Each file also has a
compact line-range index before its detailed blocks. If a block is marked
LEGACY, its explanation distinguishes the retained code from the current path.

This .txt is for reading and review. The runnable application remains in its
original .js files. This task changed documentation only, not app behavior.

Suggested first reading: customer/session storage in the main file, then its
current router (1370-1771), then the booking/management and availability helpers.
The long older appointment section is included for completeness, but can be
skipped on a first pass. Follow the block explanations before treating its old
constants, confirmation screens or Calendar calls as current behavior.

HOW A NEW BOOKING TRAVELS THROUGH THE CODE
------------------------------------------
1. Meta sends a message to POST /webhook. The signature is checked.
2. The receiving business number determines which business owns the request.
3. Reliability mode saves the input; otherwise the local in-memory queue orders it.
4. handleMessage gives menu and human support their established priority, then
   routes the text or selected action to the appropriate current helper.
5. The availability menu asks the configured Calendar about busy periods. The
   schedule/search helpers generate real available times in the business timezone.
6. A selected current time starts booking. A new customer first supplies a name;
   a known customer proceeds immediately. There is no extra confirmation screen.
7. The booking service saves a request/event identity, obtains its calendar lock,
   checks availability/ownership again and inserts the event into the exact calendar.
8. The response or reconciliation verifies the event before success is sent.
9. Reliable mode saves the outgoing result and delivers it in order. A repeated
   message or selected button refers back to the saved work rather than a new event.

SMALL GLOSSARY
---------------
Function: a named piece of work, such as validateFullName.
Session: the saved step a customer is currently answering.
Webhook: the local web address where Meta sends incoming messages/statuses.
Business scope: queries/actions include the correct business as well as customer.
Request journal: the database record of one intended Calendar operation/outcome.
Event ID: the identity of one Google Calendar appointment.
Lock: a saved reservation of the app's right to work on that calendar operation.
Reconcile: read Google to establish what happened after an unclear response.
Idempotency: repeated handling reuses one saved operation rather than adding another.
ETag: Google's event-version stamp, used to reject editing a changed source event.
Transaction: related local database updates either finish together or roll back.
async/await: wait for an operation, such as a network call, before continuing that path.
throw/catch/finally: report a problem, handle it, and run cleanup regardless of outcome.
Private extended properties: event fields that identify the owning app/business/customer.

FEATURE STATUS WHEN THIS SNAPSHOT WAS TAKEN
--------------------------------------------
The last fully owner-PC-verified baseline is v0.7.0. Versions 0.7.1-0.7.3 have
540 passing offline tests recorded for the latest code; the full combined PC
checkpoint is still pending. The owner separately passed the new Calendar-entry
display check with a screenshot. That does not by itself verify all remaining
rescheduling/WhatsApp checks. No real customer screenshot or records are copied here.

The code supports optional booking, management and reliable delivery, but the
owner's local .env controls which are enabled on the PC. The runtime fallback is
support mode; the distributed .env.example illustrates availability mode. This
review did not read the owner's actual .env, credentials, token files or database.
There is no active Services Catalog, automatic reminder workflow or operator UI.
No new feature or defect fix is implied by this explanatory review.

SAFETY BOUNDARIES EXPLAINED BY THE CODE
--------------------------------------
Database locks and request IDs protect participating app requests on this one
database/worker. Google still has no atomic availability-check-and-write with
unrelated apps or simultaneous manual edits. ETags protect the source event,
not every possible event in the target period. Unknown writes/sends require
verification; the code does not promise an exactly-once external delivery guarantee.
Backups are local, and a separate restored database does not reverse Google events.

   70 | // BUSINESS ROUTING AND SCOPED STORAGE

   73 | // ==================================================

   77 |   "businesses", "business_customers", "dev_test_business_routes",
   78 |   "business_customer_sessions", "business_support_conversations",
   79 |   "business_support_messages"

   84 | if (MANAGEMENT_ENABLED) requiredTables.push("appointment_changes");
   85 | if (RELIABILITY_ENABLED) requiredTables.push("message_inbox", "message_outbox", "delivery_worker");
   86 | const presentTables = new Set(db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'").all().map(r => r.name));
   87 | for (const table of requiredTables) {
   88 |   if (!presentTables.has(table)) throw new Error(`Phase 6A prerequisite missing: ${table}`);
   89 | }
   90 | if (!RELIABILITY_ENABLED && presentTables.has("message_inbox") && presentTables.has("message_outbox")) {
   91 |   const pending = db.prepare(`SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM message_inbox WHERE state<>'completed') OR
   92 |     EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM message_outbox WHERE state NOT IN ('accepted','expired'))) AS pending`).get().pending;
   93 |   if (pending) throw new Error("Saved message work is unresolved. Keep NUKMIRIS_ENABLE_RELIABILITY=true to recover it; compatibility mode cannot bypass it.");
   94 | }

EXPLANATION
Opens the existing SQLite file and enables its relationship checks. Required tables
depend on the enabled features. An unresolved saved inbox or reply prevents switching
reliability off and bypassing recovery.

--- F01-B06: Business identification and service selection ---

VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L95-L122

CODE
   95 | const routeQuery = db.prepare(`
   96 |   SELECT r.business_id, b.business_name
   97 |   FROM dev_test_business_routes r JOIN businesses b ON b.business_id = r.business_id
   98 |   WHERE r.sender_whatsapp_id = ? AND b.status = 'test'
   99 | `);
  100 | const receivingRoute = ROUTING_MODE === "receiving_number" ? db.prepare(`
  101 |   SELECT b.business_id, b.business_name, b.time_zone, w.phone_number_id
  102 |   FROM business_whatsapp_connections w JOIN businesses b ON b.business_id=w.business_id
  103 |   WHERE w.phone_number_id=? AND b.status IN ('test','active')
  104 | `) : null;
  105 | const businessContext = new AsyncLocalStorage();
  106 | const messageQueues = new Map();
  107 | const availability = ["availability", "booking"].includes(MODE) ? createCalendarAvailability({
  108 |   db, policy: SEARCH_POLICY,
  109 |   getClient: MODE === "booking" ? () => loadCalendarClient({ booking: true }) : undefined,
  110 | }) : null;
  111 | const bookings = MODE === "booking" ? createBookingService({ db, availability, enabled: true }) : null;
  112 | const management = MANAGEMENT_ENABLED ? createAppointmentManagement({ db, availability, enabled: true }) : null;
  113 | const appointmentLookup = bookings ? management || createAppointmentLookup({ db, availability }) : null;
  114 | function businessFor(from) {
  115 |   const context = businessContext.getStore();
  116 |   if (context?.sender === from) return context.business;
  117 |   if (ROUTING_MODE !== "development") throw new Error("A verified receiving-business context is required");
  118 |   const business = routeQuery.get(from);
  119 |   if (!business) throw new Error(`No approved Phase 6A development business mapping for sender ${from}`);
  120 |   return { ...business, phone_number_id: PHONE_ID, time_zone: ZONE };
  121 | }
  122 |

EXPLANATION
The receiving WhatsApp business number selects the business. A separate sender-based
route is retained only for explicit development mode. AsyncLocalStorage carries the
selected business while the message is processed. businessFor rejects a missing verified
business context; it does not choose a default tenant.

--- F01-B07: Conversation memory: BusinessSessions ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 123-144
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L123-L144

CODE
  123 | class BusinessSessions {
  124 |   constructor(db) {
  125 |     this.find = db.prepare("SELECT session_json FROM business_customer_sessions WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?");
  126 |     this.save = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO business_customer_sessions
  127 |       (business_id,whatsapp_id,session_json,expires_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)
  128 |       ON CONFLICT(business_id,whatsapp_id) DO UPDATE SET
  129 |       session_json=excluded.session_json, expires_at=excluded.expires_at`);
  130 |     this.remove = db.prepare("DELETE FROM business_customer_sessions WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?");
  131 |   }
  132 |   get(from) {
  133 |     const row = this.find.get(businessFor(from).business_id, from);
  134 |     return row ? JSON.parse(row.session_json) : undefined;
  135 |   }
  136 |   set(from, session) {
  137 |     if (!Number.isFinite(session.expiresAt)) throw new Error("Session requires expiration");
  138 |     this.save.run(businessFor(from).business_id, from, JSON.stringify(session), session.expiresAt);
  139 |     return this;
  140 |   }
  141 |   delete(from) { return this.remove.run(businessFor(from).business_id, from).changes > 0; }
  142 | }
  143 | const sessions = new BusinessSessions(db);
  144 |

EXPLANATION
Reads, saves and deletes the customer's current conversation step using both business ID
and WhatsApp ID. Session data is stored as JSON, with an expiry. The individual flow
checks whether an offer has expired; this class does not automatically purge old rows.

--- F01-B08: Customer identification and registration: BusinessCustomers ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 145-160
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L145-L160

  145 | class BusinessCustomers {
  146 |   constructor(db) {
  147 |     this.find = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM business_customers WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?");
  148 |     this.insert = db.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO business_customers
  149 |       (customer_id,business_id,whatsapp_id,full_name,created_at,updated_at)
  150 |       VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)`);
  151 |   }
  152 |   get(from) { return this.find.get(businessFor(from).business_id, from); }
  153 |   register(from, name) {
  154 |     const now = new Date().toISOString();
  155 |     this.insert.run(randomUUID(), businessFor(from).business_id, from, name, now, now);
  156 |     return this.get(from);
  157 |   }
  158 | }
  159 | const customers = new BusinessCustomers(db);
  160 |

EXPLANATION
Finds a customer by the combination of business and incoming WhatsApp identifier. A new
registration gets an internal random customer ID and stores the supplied name. INSERT OR
IGNORE keeps an existing registration instead of overwriting it. This block stores
names; validation happens in another block.

--- F01-B09: Human-support conversation storage ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 161-194
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L161-L194

CODE
  161 | class BusinessHumanSupport {
  162 |   constructor(db) {
  163 |     this.find = db.prepare("SELECT status FROM business_support_conversations WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?");
  164 |     this.openStatement = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO business_support_conversations
  165 |       (business_id,whatsapp_id,customer_id,status,assigned_to,created_at,updated_at)
  166 |       VALUES (?,?,?,'waiting',NULL,?,?)
  167 |       ON CONFLICT(business_id,whatsapp_id) DO UPDATE SET
  168 |       status='waiting', customer_id=excluded.customer_id, updated_at=excluded.updated_at`);
  169 |     this.closeStatement = db.prepare(`UPDATE business_support_conversations SET status='closed', updated_at=?
  170 |       WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?`);
  171 |     this.messageStatement = db.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO business_support_messages
  172 |       (id,business_id,whatsapp_id,direction,message_text,created_at)
  173 |       VALUES (?,?,?,'incoming',?,?)`);
  174 |     this.touchStatement = db.prepare(`UPDATE business_support_conversations
  175 |       SET status='waiting', updated_at=? WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?`);
  176 |   }
  177 |   isOpen(from) {
  178 |     const status = this.find.get(businessFor(from).business_id, from)?.status;
  179 |     return status === 'waiting' || status === 'in_progress';
  180 |   }
  181 |   open(from, customerId) {
  182 |     const now = new Date().toISOString();
  183 |     this.openStatement.run(businessFor(from).business_id, from, customerId || null, now, now);
  184 |   }
  185 |   saveMessage(from, id, content) {
  186 |     const businessId = businessFor(from).business_id;
  187 |     const now = new Date().toISOString();
  188 |     this.messageStatement.run(id || randomUUID(), businessId, from, content, now);
  189 |     this.touchStatement.run(now, businessId, from);
  190 |   }
  191 |   close(from) {
  192 |     this.closeStatement.run(new Date().toISOString(), businessFor(from).business_id, from);
  193 |   }
  194 | }

EXPLANATION
Opens or closes a support conversation and saves incoming text under the same
business/customer pair. Waiting or in-progress status means support owns the
conversation. This is local conversation storage; it does not provide a staffed operator
dashboard.

--- F01-B14: Normalize a WhatsApp recipient for sending ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 259-267
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L259-L267

CODE
  259 | // Normalize Mexican WhatsApp incoming identifiers for outbound messages.
  260 | // Keep the original incoming identifier unchanged for SQLite lookups.
  261 | function normalizeRecipient(phone) {
  262 |   const recipient = String(phone);
  263 |   return /^521\d{10}$/.test(recipient)
  264 |     ? "52" + recipient.slice(3)
  265 |     : recipient;
  266 | }
  267 |

EXPLANATION
Converts the historical Mexican 521-plus-ten-digits identifier to 52-plus-ten-digits for
delivery. The database lookup keeps the original incoming identifier, so display/sending
normalization does not change customer ownership.

--- F01-B15: LEGACY: readable phone display for the older registration wizard ---

CODE
  268 | // Format the incoming WhatsApp identifier for display.
  269 | // The original identifier remains the database lookup key.
  270 | function displayWhatsAppNumber(from) {
  271 |   const digits = String(from).replace(/\D/g, "");
  272 |
  273 |   const normalized = /^521\d{10}$/.test(digits)
  274 |     ? "52" + digits.slice(3)
  275 |     : digits;
  276 |
  277 |   if (/^52\d{10}$/.test(normalized)) {
  278 |     return (
  279 |       "+52 " +
  280 |       normalized.slice(2, 4) + " " +
  281 |       normalized.slice(4, 8) + " " +
  282 |       normalized.slice(8)
  283 |     );
  284 |   }
  285 |
  286 |   return "+" + normalized;
  287 | }
  288 |

EXPLANATION
Formats an identifier with an international plus sign and spaces for Mexican numbers.
Its callers are in the older registration/event helpers. New Calendar descriptions use
the existing scoped customer record in lib/booking.js.

--- F01-B16: Validate a customer name ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 289-303
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L289-L303

CODE
  289 | function validateFullName(input) {
  290 |   const name = input.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  291 |
  292 |   if (
  293 |     name.length < 3 ||
  294 |     name.length > 120 ||
  295 |     !/\p{L}/u.test(name) ||
  296 |     /[0-9<>]/.test(name)
  297 |   ) {
  298 |     return null;
  299 |   }
  300 |
  301 |   return name;
  302 | }
  303 |

EXPLANATION
Collapses repeated whitespace and trims the name. Rejects fewer than 3 or more than 120
characters, no letter, digits, or angle brackets. A rejected name returns null. The code
does not enforce a particular number of name parts or require two surnames.

--- F01-B17: Build and send WhatsApp messages ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 304-347
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L304-L347

CODE
  304 | // ==================================================
  305 | // WHATSAPP MESSAGE SENDING
  306 | // ==================================================
  307 |
  308 | function textPayload(to, body) {
  309 |   return { messaging_product: "whatsapp", recipient_type: "individual", to: normalizeRecipient(to),
  310 |     type: "text", text: { preview_url: false, body } };
  311 | }
  312 | async function sendPayloads(to, payloads) {
  313 |   if (delivery) {
  314 |     const inboxId = businessContext.getStore()?.inboxId;
  315 |     if (!inboxId) throw new Error("Reliable replies require a persisted incoming message");
  316 |     delivery.outputs(inboxId, payloads);
  317 |     return { queued: true };
  318 |   }
  319 |   let data;
  320 |   for (const payload of payloads) {
  321 |     const response = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/${API_VERSION}/${businessFor(to).phone_number_id}/messages`, {
  322 |       method: "POST", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${TOKEN}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  323 |     });
  324 |     data = await response.json();
  325 |     if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`WhatsApp API: ${JSON.stringify(data)}`);
  326 |     console.log("WhatsApp message accepted by Meta.");
  327 |   }
  328 |   return data;
  329 | }
  330 | async function sendMessage(to, body) { return sendPayloads(to, [textPayload(to, body)]); }
  331 | async function sendReplyButtons(to, body, buttons, precedingTexts = []) {
  332 |   if (!buttons.length || buttons.length > 3) throw new Error("WhatsApp supports 1 to 3 reply buttons.");
  333 |   if (buttons.some(button => !button.title || button.title.length > 20)) throw new Error("WhatsApp button title must be 1 to 20 characters.");
  334 |   return sendPayloads(to, [...precedingTexts.map(text => textPayload(to, text)), {
  335 |     messaging_product: "whatsapp", recipient_type: "individual", to: normalizeRecipient(to), type: "interactive",
  336 |     interactive: { type: "button", body: { text: body }, action: {
  337 |       buttons: buttons.map(button => ({ type: "reply", reply: { id: button.id, title: button.title } })),
  338 |     } },
  339 |   }]);
  340 | }
  341 | async function sendAppointmentList(to, body, rows, button = "Ver citas", sectionTitle = "Mis citas") {
  342 |   if (rows.length < 1 || rows.length > 10) throw new Error("WhatsApp list must contain 1-10 rows.");
  343 |   return sendPayloads(to, [{ messaging_product: "whatsapp", recipient_type: "individual", to: normalizeRecipient(to), type: "interactive",
  344 |     interactive: { type: "list", body: { text: body }, action: { button, sections: [{ title: sectionTitle, rows }] } },
  345 |   }]);
  346 | }
  347 |

EXPLANATION
Creates text, button and list payloads. Button replies are limited to three choices with
titles at most 20 characters; lists allow up to ten rows. Reliable mode saves outgoing
payloads instead of sending immediately. Compatibility mode sends directly through the
selected business's Meta phone-number ID.

  361 | // ==================================================
  362 | // SIMPLIFIED THREE-BUTTON MAIN MENU
  363 | // ==================================================
  364 | async function showMainMenu(from) {
  365 |   const customer = customers.get(from);
  366 |   const business = businessFor(from);
  367 |   const firstLine = customer
  368 |     ? `Hola ${customer.full_name}, bienvenido a ${business.business_name}.`
  369 |     : `Hola, bienvenido a ${business.business_name}.`;
  370 |   let details = "";
  371 |   if (customer && appointmentLookup) {
  372 |     try {
  373 |       const appointments = await appointmentLookup.listAppointments({ businessId: business.business_id, sender: from });
  374 |       details = appointmentOverview(appointments, availability.configuration(business.business_id).settings.timeZone);
  375 |     } catch {
  376 |       console.error("Menu appointment lookup failed; no appointment status was assumed.");
  377 |       details = "Por ahora no pude consultar tus citas. Puedes intentar de nuevo más tarde.";
  378 |     }
  379 |   }
  380 |   const actions = '¿En qué podemos ayudarte?\n\n' +
  381 |     'Escribe "cita" para agendar una cita nueva, "reagendar" para cambiar tu cita o "cancelar" para cancelarla.\n\n' +
  382 |     "O escribe tu mensaje para hablar con una persona.";
  383 |   let body = [firstLine, details, actions].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
  384 |   const menuId = randomUUID();
  385 |   sessions.set(from, {
  386 |     mode: "main_menu", menuId, expiresAt: Date.now() + OFFER_LIFETIME
  387 |   });
  388 |   try {
  389 |     const precedingTexts = body.length > 1024 ? textChunks([firstLine, details].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n")) : [];
  390 |     if (precedingTexts.length) body = actions;
  391 |     await sendReplyButtons(from,
  392 |       body,
  393 |       [
  394 |         { id: `menu:${menuId}:book`, title: "Agendar una cita" },
  395 |         { id: `menu:${menuId}:manage`, title: "Reagendar / cancelar" },
  396 |         { id: `menu:${menuId}:human`, title: "Hablar con alguien" }
  397 |       ], precedingTexts
  398 |     );
  399 |   } catch (error) {
  400 |     sessions.delete(from);
  401 |     throw error;
  402 |   }
  403 | }
  404 |

EXPLANATION
Greets a registered customer by name and can show their owned upcoming appointments. A
failed lookup produces an unavailable-information message rather than claiming no
appointments exist. Each menu gets a fresh random identity and expiry; long appointment
overviews are split before the buttons.

--- F01-B24: LEGACY: old three-slot search ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 541-606
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L541-L606

CODE
  541 | // ==================================================
  542 | // FIND THREE AVAILABLE SLOTS
  543 | // ==================================================
  544 |
  545 | async function findSlots() {
  546 |   const now = DateTime.now().setZone(ZONE);
  547 |   const until = now.plus({ days: 8 });
  548 |
  549 |   const busy = await getBusyPeriods(
  550 |     now.toISO(),
  551 |     until.toISO()
  552 |   );
  553 |
  554 |   const slots = [];
  555 |
  556 |   for (let day = 0; day < 8; day++) {
  557 |     const date = now.startOf("day").plus({ days: day });
  558 |
  559 |     if (date.weekday > 5) continue;
  560 |
  561 |     for (const hour of HOURS) {
  562 |       const start = date.set({
  563 |         hour,
  564 |         minute: 0,
  565 |         second: 0,
  566 |         millisecond: 0
  567 |       });
  568 |
  569 |       const end = start.plus({ hours: 1 });
  570 |
  571 |       if (start <= now || end > until) continue;
  572 |
  573 |       const slot = {
  574 |         start: start.toISO(),
  575 |         end: end.toISO()
  576 |       };
  577 |
  578 |       const overlaps = busy.some(period => {
  579 |         const first = DateTime
  580 |           .fromISO(period.start)
  581 |           .toMillis();
  582 |
  583 |         const last = DateTime
  584 |           .fromISO(period.end)
  585 |           .toMillis();
  586 |
  587 |         return (
  588 |           start.toMillis() < last &&
  589 |           end.toMillis() > first
  590 |         );
  591 |       });
  592 |
  593 |       if (
  594 |         !overlaps &&
  595 |         !lockedSlots.has(keyFor(slot))
  596 |       ) {
  597 |         slots.push(slot);
  598 |       }
  599 |
  600 |       if (slots.length === 3) return slots;
  601 |     }
  602 |   }
  603 |
  604 |   return slots;
  605 | }
  606 |

EXPLANATION
This historical search walks days and the old HOURS constant. It depends on the blocked
Calendar query. It is not the current full-schedule or dynamic-horizon search; those
live in lib/availability.js and lib/availability-search.js.

  770 | // ==================================================
  771 | // RESCHEDULE EXISTING APPOINTMENT
  772 | // ==================================================
  773 |
  774 | async function rescheduleAppointment(from, session, slot) {
  775 |   throw new Error("Calendar actions disabled during Phase 6A.4 tenant isolation tests");
  776 |   const eventId = session.originalEventId;
  777 |
  778 |   if (!eventId) {
  779 |     throw new Error("No original appointment was selected.");
  780 |   }
  781 |
  782 |   if (lockedEvents.has(eventId)) {
  783 |     throw new Error(
  784 |       "This appointment is already being updated."
  785 |     );
  786 |   }
  787 |
  788 |   lockedEvents.add(eventId);
  789 |
  790 |   try {
  791 |     const response = await calendar.events.get({
  792 |       calendarId: CALENDAR_ID,
  793 |       eventId
  794 |     });

  796 |     const original = response.data;
  797 |
  798 |     if (
  799 |       original.status === "cancelled" ||
  800 |       original.extendedProperties?.private
  801 |         ?.nukmirisCustomerId !== from ||
  802 |       !original.start?.dateTime
  803 |     ) {
  804 |       throw new Error("Appointment no longer valid.");
  805 |     }
  806 |
  807 |     const currentStart = DateTime
  808 |       .fromISO(original.start.dateTime)
  809 |       .toMillis();
  810 |
  811 |     const offeredStart = DateTime
  812 |       .fromISO(session.originalStart)
  813 |       .toMillis();
  814 |
  815 |     if (currentStart !== offeredStart) {
  816 |       throw new Error(
  817 |         "Appointment changed since the offer was made."
  818 |       );
  819 |     }
  820 |
  821 |     // Preserve the previously tested lock correction.
  822 |     if (!(await isFreeOnCalendar(slot))) {
  823 |       console.log(
  824 |         "New appointment time is no longer available."
  825 |       );
  826 |
  827 |       return null;
  828 |     }
  829 |
  830 |     const updated = await calendar.events.patch({
  831 |       calendarId: CALENDAR_ID,
  832 |       eventId,
  833 |
  834 |       requestBody: {
  835 |         start: {
  836 |           dateTime: slot.start,
  837 |           timeZone: ZONE
  838 |         },
  839 |
  840 |         end: {
  841 |           dateTime: slot.end,
  842 |           timeZone: ZONE
  843 |         }
  844 |       },
  845 |
  846 |       sendUpdates: "none"
  847 |     });
  848 |
  849 |     console.log("APPOINTMENT RESCHEDULED SUCCESSFULLY");
  850 |     console.log("Google Event ID:", updated.data.id);
  851 |
  852 |     return updated.data;
  853 |
  854 |   } finally {
  855 |     lockedEvents.delete(eventId);
  856 |   }
  857 | }
  858 |

  859 | // ==================================================
  860 | // PROCESS APPOINTMENT SELECTION
  861 | // ==================================================
  862 |
  863 | async function selectSlot(from, choice) {
  864 |   const session = sessions.get(from);
  865 |
  866 |   if (!session) return;
  867 |
  868 |   sessions.delete(from);
  869 |
  870 |   if (Date.now() > session.expiresAt) {
  871 |     await sendMessage(
  872 |       from,
  873 |       "Los horarios han vencido. " +
  874 |       "Escribe Cita o Reagendar para consultar nuevamente."
  875 |     );
  876 |
  877 |     return;
  878 |   }
  879 |
  880 |   const slot = session.slots[Number(choice) - 1];
  881 |
  882 |   if (!slot) {
  883 |     await sendMessage(
  884 |       from,
  885 |       "Selecciona un horario válido."
  886 |     );
  887 |     return;
  888 |   }
  889 |
  890 |   if (DateTime.fromISO(slot.start).toMillis() <= Date.now()) {
  891 |     await sendMessage(
  892 |       from,
  893 |       "Ese horario ya pasó. Consulta nuevos horarios."
  894 |     );
  895 |     return;
  896 |   }
  897 |
  898 |   const lockKey = keyFor(slot);
  899 |
  900 |   if (lockedSlots.has(lockKey)) {
  901 |     await sendMessage(
  902 |       from,
  903 |       "Ese horario está siendo reservado. " +
  904 |       "Escribe Cita o Reagendar para consultar otros."
  905 |     );
  906 |
  907 |     return;
  908 |   }
  909 |
  910 |   lockedSlots.add(lockKey);
  911 |
  912 |   let calendarWriteAttempted = false;
  913 |
  914 |   try {
  915 |     if (!(await isFreeOnCalendar(slot))) {
  916 |       await sendMessage(
  917 |         from,
  918 |         "Ese horario ya no está disponible. " +
  919 |         "Consulta nuevos horarios."
  920 |       );
  921 |
  922 |       return;
  923 |     }
  924 |
  925 |     if (session.mode === "reschedule") {
  926 |       calendarWriteAttempted = true;
  927 |
  928 |       const updated = await rescheduleAppointment(
  929 |         from,
  930 |         session,
  931 |         slot
  932 |       );
  933 |
  934 |       if (!updated) {
  935 |         calendarWriteAttempted = false;
  936 |
  937 |         await sendMessage(
  938 |           from,
  939 |           "Ese horario dejó de estar disponible. " +
  940 |           "Escribe Reagendar para consultar otros."
  941 |         );
  942 |
  943 |         return;
  944 |       }
  945 |
  946 |       await sendMessage(
  947 |         from,
  948 |         "¡Tu cita fue reagendada exitosamente!\n\n" +
  949 |         "Nueva fecha y hora:\n" +
  950 |         formatTime(slot.start)
  951 |       );
  952 |
  953 |     } else {
  954 |       calendarWriteAttempted = true;
  955 |
  956 |       await createAppointment(from, slot);
  957 |
  958 |       await sendMessage(
  959 |         from,
  960 |         "¡Tu cita está confirmada!\n\n" +
  961 |         "Fecha y hora:\n" +
  962 |         formatTime(slot.start) +
  963 |         "\n\n¡Te esperamos!"
  964 |       );

  966 |

  971 |     );
  972 |
  973 |     if (calendarWriteAttempted) {

  977 |         "a WhatsApp confirmation."
  978 |       );

--- F01-B35: Compatibility-mode duplicate detection ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1348-1369
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L1348-L1369

CODE
 1348 | // ==================================================
 1349 | // DUPLICATE WHATSAPP MESSAGE DETECTION
 1350 | // ==================================================
 1351 |
 1352 | function alreadyProcessed(id) {
 1353 |   if (!id) return false;
 1354 |
 1355 |   const now = Date.now();
 1356 |
 1357 |   for (const [key, timestamp] of processedMessages) {
 1358 |     if (now - timestamp > 60 * 60 * 1000) {
 1359 |       processedMessages.delete(key);
 1360 |     }
 1361 |   }
 1362 |
 1363 |   if (processedMessages.has(id)) return true;
 1364 |
 1365 |   processedMessages.set(id, now);
 1366 |
 1367 |   return false;
 1368 | }
 1369 |

EXPLANATION
Remembers processed message IDs for about one hour in memory, scoped by business when
called by the router. This record disappears on process restart. Reliability mode uses
the persistent inbox instead.

--- F01-B40: Execute or check a booking and report its outcome ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1451-1485
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L1451-L1485

CODE
 1451 | async function handleBookingDecision(from, command) {
 1452 |   const match = /^booking:([0-9a-f-]{36}):(yes|no|retry|status)$/.exec(command);
 1453 |   if (!match) {
 1454 |     await sendMessage(from, "Esta opción ya no está vigente. Escribe Cita para comenzar nuevamente.");
 1455 |     return;
 1456 |   }
 1457 |   saveContinuation("booking", match[1], match[2]);
 1458 |   const context = { businessId: businessFor(from).business_id, sender: from, requestId: match[1] };
 1459 |   const outcome = match[2] === "status" ? await bookings.reconcile(context)
 1460 |     : match[2] === "no" ? bookings.decline(context) : await bookings.confirm(context);
 1461 |   if (outcome.status === "busy") {
 1462 |     await sendReplyButtons(from, "Estamos comprobando otra reserva. Intenta de nuevo en unos momentos.",
 1463 |       [{ id: `booking:${match[1]}:retry`, title: "Reintentar" }]);
 1464 |     return;
 1465 |   }
 1466 |   if (["uncertain", "pending"].includes(outcome.status)) {
 1467 |     console.error("Booking needs verification. Request ID:", match[1]);
 1468 |     await sendReplyButtons(from, "Tu solicitud sigue en verificación. Consulta su estado antes de intentar otra reserva.",
 1469 |       [{ id: `booking:${match[1]}:status`, title: "Consultar estado" }]);
 1470 |     return;
 1471 |   }
 1472 |   const current = sessions.get(from);
 1473 |   if (current?.requestId === match[1] && current.mode !== "booking_result") sessions.delete(from);
 1474 |   if (outcome.status === "confirmed") {
 1475 |     const zone = availability.configuration(context.businessId).settings.timeZone;
 1476 |     await sendMessage(from, appointmentSuccess({ start: outcome.start, end: outcome.end, zone, businessName: businessFor(from).business_name }));
 1477 |   } else if (outcome.status === "rejected") {
 1478 |     await offerAvailability(from, { notice: "No pude reservar ese horario. Te muestro opciones actualizadas." });
 1479 |   } else if (outcome.status === "declined" && match[2] === "no") {
 1480 |     await offerAvailability(from);
 1481 |   } else {
 1482 |     await sendMessage(from, "No pudimos confirmar esta solicitud. Escribe Cita para consultar nuevos horarios.");
 1483 |   }
 1484 | }
 1485 |

EXPLANATION
The internal yes/retry/status/no decision works on a saved request. It is not a new
customer confirmation screen. Success is sent only for a verified confirmed event; busy
or uncertain results receive retry/status guidance, and rejected times receive fresh
options.

 1486 | async function handleMessage(message) {
 1487 |   const isText = message.type === "text";

 1490 |   const isList = message.type === "interactive" &&

 1503 |     console.log("Customer operation already in progress.");
 1504 |     return;
 1505 |   }
 1506 |   busyCustomers.add(customerKey);
 1507 |   try {
 1508 |     const business = businessFor(from); // Fail closed for unassigned test numbers.

comparison text is normalized; expected names and support text keep their original

--- F01-B56: Receive signed webhook messages and preserve their order ---
FILE: backend/whatsapp-webhook.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1790-1836
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/whatsapp-webhook.js#L1790-L1836

CODE
 1790 | // ==================================================
 1791 | // RECEIVE WHATSAPP WEBHOOK EVENTS
 1792 | // ==================================================
 1793 |
 1794 | app.post("/webhook", (req, res) => {
 1795 |   if (!validMetaSignature(req.rawBody, req.get("X-Hub-Signature-256"), APP_SECRET)) {
 1796 |     return res.sendStatus(403);
 1797 |   }
 1798 |   if (delivery) {
 1799 |     try { delivery.accept(req.body); }
 1800 |     catch (error) {
 1801 |       console.error("Webhook was not acknowledged because inbox persistence failed.");
 1802 |       return res.sendStatus(error.status || 503);
 1803 |     }
 1804 |     res.sendStatus(200);
 1805 |     deliveryWorker?.wake();
 1806 |     return;
 1807 |   }
 1808 |   res.sendStatus(200);
 1809 |
 1810 |   const changes =
 1811 |     req.body?.entry?.flatMap(
 1812 |       entry => entry.changes || []
 1813 |     ) || [];
 1814 |
 1815 |   for (const change of changes) {
 1816 |     const messages = change.value?.messages || [];
 1817 |
 1818 |     for (const message of messages) {
 1819 |       if (typeof message.from !== "string" || !message.from) continue;
 1820 |       const phoneId = change.value?.metadata?.phone_number_id;
 1821 |       const business = ROUTING_MODE === "receiving_number"
 1822 |         ? (typeof phoneId === "string" ? receivingRoute.get(phoneId) : undefined)
 1823 |         : routeQuery.get(message.from);
 1824 |       if (!business) continue;
 1825 |       const resolved = { ...business, phone_number_id: business.phone_number_id || PHONE_ID };
 1826 |       const key = `${resolved.business_id}:${message.from}`;
 1827 |       const queued = (messageQueues.get(key) || Promise.resolve()).catch(() => {}).then(() =>
 1828 |         businessContext.run({ business: resolved, sender: message.from }, () => handleMessage(message)));
 1829 |       messageQueues.set(key, queued);
 1830 |       queued.catch(() => console.error("Webhook processing failed; inspect the local configuration.")).finally(() => {
 1831 |         if (messageQueues.get(key) === queued) messageQueues.delete(key);
 1832 |       });
 1833 |     }
 1834 |   }
 1835 | });
 1836 |

EXPLANATION
POST /webhook rejects an invalid Meta signature. Reliable mode saves the input before
acknowledging and wakes the worker. Compatibility mode acknowledges first and queues
messages per business/customer in memory. Unknown receiving business numbers are
ignored.

 1842 |   try {
 1843 |     if (delivery) {
 1844 |       db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA synchronous=FULL; PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000");
 1845 |       delivery.acquire();
 1846 |       backupSchedule = createBackupSchedule({ db,
 1847 |         directory: path.resolve(__dirname, process.env.NUKMIRIS_BACKUP_DIR || ".local/backups"),
 1848 |         intervalHours: Number(process.env.NUKMIRIS_BACKUP_INTERVAL_HOURS || 24),
 1849 |         keep: Number(process.env.NUKMIRIS_BACKUP_KEEP || 7),
 1850 |         onError: () => console.error("Automatic database backup failed; inspect reliability status."),
 1851 |       });
 1852 |       await backupSchedule.ensure();
 1853 |     }
 1854 |     if (bookings) await bookings.verify();
 1855 |     if (management) await management.verify();
 1856 |     if (availability) {
 1857 |       const connected = db.prepare("SELECT business_id FROM business_whatsapp_connections").all();
 1858 |       if (!connected.length) throw new Error("Configure a business connection before starting availability mode");
 1859 |       for (const business of connected) await availability.verify(business.business_id);
 1860 |       console.log(bookings ? "Google Calendar booking authorization and availability verified."
 1861 |         : "Google Calendar availability connection verified. Event changes remain disabled.");
 1862 |     }
 1863 |
 1864 |     if (delivery) {
 1865 |       deliveryWorker = createDeliveryWorker({ delivery,
 1866 |         handle: input => handleMessage(input.message),
 1867 |         resume: input => {
 1868 |           const { kind, requestId, decision } = input.continuation;
 1869 |           if (kind === "booking" && bookings) return handleBookingDecision(input.sender, `booking:${requestId}:${decision}`);
 1870 |           if (kind === "management" && managementMenu) return managementMenu.resume(input.sender, requestId, decision);
 1871 |           throw new Error("The recorded operation cannot run with this configuration");
 1872 |         },
 1873 |         runInContext: (input, action) => businessContext.run({ business: input.business, sender: input.sender, inboxId: input.inbox_id }, action),
 1874 |         send: row => sendStoredReply({ row, token: TOKEN, apiVersion: API_VERSION }),
 1875 |         onError: () => console.error("Reliable delivery retained work for retry or review."),
 1876 |       });
 1877 |     }
 1878 |     return app.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
 1879 |       if (delivery) {
 1880 |         deliveryWorker.start();
 1881 |         deliveryWorker.wake();
 1882 |         backupSchedule.start();
 1883 |       }
 1884 |       console.log("--------------------------------");
 1885 |       console.log("NukMiris WhatsApp + Google Calendar POC");
 1886 |       console.log("--------------------------------");
 1887 |       console.log("Webhook server running on port", PORT);
 1888 |       console.log(bookings ? "Google Calendar: confirmed new bookings" : availability ? "Google Calendar: read-only availability" : "Google Calendar: DISABLED");
 1889 |       console.log(delivery ? "Reliable delivery: persisted inbox/outbox and automatic backups enabled" : "Reliable delivery: disabled");
 1890 |       console.log("Persistent sessions: SQLite enabled");
 1891 |       console.log("Customer registry: SQLite enabled");
 1892 |       console.log(availability ? "Availability lookup: Enabled" : "Availability lookup: Disabled");
 1893 |       console.log(bookings ? "New booking: selecting a slot books the appointment" : "New booking: DISABLED for this checkpoint");
 1894 |       console.log(management ? "Appointment rescheduling: selecting a new slot applies the change" : "Appointment rescheduling: DISABLED for this checkpoint");
 1895 |       console.log(management ? "Appointment cancellation: selecting an appointment cancels it" : "Appointment cancellation: DISABLED for this checkpoint");
 1896 |       console.log("Booking trigger: cita");
 1897 |       console.log("Rescheduling trigger: reagendar");
 1898 |       console.log("Cancellation trigger: cancelar");
 1899 |       console.log("--------------------------------");
 1900 |       console.log("Waiting for WhatsApp messages...");
 1901 |     });
 1902 |
 1903 |   } catch (error) {
 1904 |     await deliveryWorker?.stop();
 1905 |     await backupSchedule?.stop();
 1906 |     delivery?.release();
 1907 |     console.error("Startup failed:", error.message);
 1908 |     process.exit(1);
 1909 |   }
 1910 | }
 1911 |

EXPLANATION
Reliable mode acquires one local worker owner, verifies a backup, checks configured
Google permissions/calendars and connects recovery callbacks. The server then listens,
starts delivery/backup scheduling and logs enabled capabilities. A startup failure stops
workers and releases ownership before exiting.

--- F02-B02: SETUP ONLY: booking journal and calendar lock tables ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 8-41
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L8-L41

CODE
    8 | function prepareBookingSchema(db) {
    9 |   db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON; BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
   10 |   try {
   11 |     db.exec(`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_business_customer_identity
   12 |       ON business_customers(business_id,customer_id);
   13 |     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS booking_requests (
   14 |       request_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   15 |       business_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   16 |       sender_whatsapp_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   17 |       customer_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   18 |       customer_name TEXT NOT NULL,
   19 |       calendar_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   20 |       slot_start TEXT NOT NULL,
   21 |       slot_end TEXT NOT NULL,
   22 |       start_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,
   23 |       end_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,
   24 |       event_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   25 |       state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('offered','pending','confirmed','rejected','uncertain','declined','removed')),
   26 |       expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   27 |       created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   28 |       updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   29 |       UNIQUE(calendar_id,event_id),
   30 |       FOREIGN KEY(business_id,customer_id) REFERENCES business_customers(business_id,customer_id)
   31 |     );
   32 |     CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_booking_calendar_time ON booking_requests(calendar_id,start_ms,end_ms,state);
   33 |     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS calendar_booking_locks (
   34 |       calendar_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   35 |       request_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES booking_requests(request_id),
   36 |       created_at TEXT NOT NULL
   37 |     );`);
   38 |     db.exec("COMMIT");
   39 |   } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
   40 | }
   41 |

EXPLANATION
Creates the durable booking-request record and one lock row per calendar when the
explicit preparation command is used. The foreign key binds each booking to the
business/customer identity. Normal app startup checks these tables instead of silently
creating them.

--- F02-B03: Create a booking service and scoped request lookup ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 42-56
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L42-L56

CODE
   42 | function createBookingService({ db, availability, enabled = false,
   43 |   getClient = () => loadCalendarClient({ booking: true }),
   44 |   verifyAuthorization = verifyBookingAuthorization, now = () => DateTime.now(), lifetimeMs = 15 * 60 * 1000 }) {
   45 |   let client;
   46 |   let authorization;
   47 |   async function calendar() { return client || (client = await getClient()); }
   48 |   function authorize() {
   49 |     if (!authorization) authorization = Promise.resolve().then(verifyAuthorization).catch(error => { authorization = undefined; throw error; });
   50 |     return authorization;
   51 |   }
   52 |   const scoped = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM booking_requests WHERE request_id=? AND business_id=? AND sender_whatsapp_id=?");
   53 |   function lookup({ requestId, businessId, sender }) { return scoped.get(requestId, businessId, sender); }
   54 |   function result(row, status = row.state) {
   55 |     return { status, requestId: row.request_id, eventId: row.event_id, start: row.slot_start, end: row.slot_end, name: row.customer_name };
   56 |   }

EXPLANATION
Builds a service for an existing database and availability source. Calendar
authorization is checked and reused after success. Looking up a request always includes
request ID, business ID and sender; knowing somebody else's request ID alone is
insufficient.

--- F02-B04: Update results and release only the matching lock ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 57-75
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L57-L75

CODE
   57 |   function update(row, state) {
   58 |     db.prepare("UPDATE booking_requests SET state=?,updated_at=? WHERE request_id=?").run(state, now().toISO(), row.request_id);
   59 |     row.state = state;
   60 |   }
   61 |   function release(row) {
   62 |     db.prepare("DELETE FROM calendar_booking_locks WHERE calendar_id=? AND request_id=?").run(row.calendar_id, row.request_id);
   63 |   }
   64 |   function terminal(row, state) {
   65 |     db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
   66 |     try {
   67 |       const current = lookup({ requestId: row.request_id, businessId: row.business_id, sender: row.sender_whatsapp_id });
   68 |       // Recovery reads may finish late, after a confirmed appointment has
   69 |       // entered another operation. Never release that newer operation's lock.
   70 |       if (!["pending", "uncertain"].includes(current.state)) { db.exec("COMMIT"); return result(current); }
   71 |       update(row, state); release(row); db.exec("COMMIT");
   72 |     }
   73 |     catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
   74 |     return result(row);
   75 |   }

EXPLANATION
Stores state changes and releases a lock only for its own request. terminal rereads the
record inside a database transaction, so a late recovery result cannot overwrite a newer
operation or release that newer lock.

--- F02-B05: Verify calendar mapping, event ownership and times ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 76-89
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L76-L89

CODE
   76 |   function mappingMatches(row) {
   77 |     return availability.configuration(row.business_id).calendarId === row.calendar_id;
   78 |   }
   79 |   function owns(event, row) {
   80 |     const fields = event.extendedProperties?.private;
   81 |     return event.id === row.event_id && fields?.nukmirisBusinessId === row.business_id &&
   82 |       fields?.nukmirisBookingRequestId === row.request_id && fields?.nukmirisCustomerId === row.sender_whatsapp_id &&
   83 |       fields?.nukmirisInternalCustomerId === row.customer_id;
   84 |   }
   85 |   function matches(event, row) {
   86 |     return owns(event, row) && event.status !== "cancelled" &&
   87 |       DateTime.fromISO(event.start?.dateTime || "").toMillis() === row.start_ms &&
   88 |       DateTime.fromISO(event.end?.dateTime || "").toMillis() === row.end_ms;
   89 |   }

EXPLANATION
Checks the exact calendar and private event properties against the saved business,
sender, internal customer and request. A matching title is not proof of ownership; the
event ID and start/end times also have to match.

--- F02-B06: Reconcile an uncertain insertion ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 90-102
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L90-L102

CODE
   90 |   async function reconcile(context) {
   91 |     const row = lookup(context);
   92 |     if (!row) return { status: "invalid" };
   93 |     if (!enabled) return { status: "disabled" };
   94 |     if (row.state === "confirmed") return result(row);
   95 |     if (!["pending", "uncertain"].includes(row.state)) return result(row);
   96 |     if (!mappingMatches(row)) return result(row, "uncertain");
   97 |     try {
   98 |       const response = await (await calendar()).events.get({ calendarId: row.calendar_id, eventId: row.event_id });
   99 |       if (matches(response.data, row)) return terminal(row, "confirmed");
  100 |     } catch { /* Missing/failed reads do not prove that an ambiguous insert never happened. */ }
  101 |     return result(row, "uncertain");
  102 |   }

EXPLANATION
Reads the original saved event ID from Google. If ownership and times prove the booking
exists, marks it confirmed. A failed or missing read leaves it uncertain rather than
creating another appointment.

--- F02-B07: Invalidate unused earlier offers ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 103-106
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L103-L106

CODE
  103 |   function invalidateOffers({ businessId, sender }) {
  104 |     db.prepare("UPDATE booking_requests SET state='declined',updated_at=? WHERE business_id=? AND sender_whatsapp_id=? AND state='offered'")
  105 |       .run(now().toISO(), businessId, sender);
  106 |   }

EXPLANATION
Declines offered requests for this business and sender only. It does not decline
confirmed, pending or uncertain work.

--- F02-B08: Create the local booking request ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 107-127
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L107-L127

CODE
  107 |   function createIntent({ businessId, sender, slot, calendarId }) {
  108 |     if (!enabled) throw new Error("Automatic booking is disabled");
  109 |     const current = availability.configuration(businessId);
  110 |     if (current.calendarId !== calendarId) throw new Error("The selected calendar changed");
  111 |     const customer = db.prepare("SELECT customer_id,full_name FROM business_customers WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?").get(businessId, sender);
  112 |     if (!customer) throw new Error("A business-scoped customer registration is required");
  113 |     const start = DateTime.fromISO(slot.start), end = DateTime.fromISO(slot.end);
  114 |     if (!start.isValid || !end.isValid || start <= now() || end <= start) throw new Error("The selected appointment time is invalid");
  115 |     const requestId = randomUUID();
  116 |     const eventId = createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify([businessId, calendarId, requestId])).digest("hex");
  117 |     db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
  118 |     try {
  119 |       invalidateOffers({ businessId, sender });
  120 |       db.prepare(`INSERT INTO booking_requests VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'offered',?,?,?)`)
  121 |         .run(requestId, businessId, sender, customer.customer_id, customer.full_name, calendarId,
  122 |           slot.start, slot.end, start.toMillis(), end.toMillis(), eventId,
  123 |           now().toMillis() + lifetimeMs, now().toISO(), now().toISO());
  124 |       db.exec("COMMIT");
  125 |     } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
  126 |     return result(lookup({ requestId, businessId, sender }));
  127 |   }

EXPLANATION
Uses the stored scoped customer name, selected slot and exact calendar to create a
request. It derives a stable event ID from that request and saves the full times. This
function records intent locally; it does not insert a Google event.

--- F02-B09: Claim the request and calendar lock ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 128-143
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L128-L143

CODE
  128 |   function claim(context) {
  129 |     db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
  130 |     try {
  131 |       const row = lookup(context);
  132 |       if (!row) { db.exec("COMMIT"); return { status: "invalid" }; }
  133 |       if (row.state !== "offered") { db.exec("COMMIT"); return result(row); }
  134 |       if (row.expires_at < now().toMillis()) { update(row, "rejected"); db.exec("COMMIT"); return result(row, "expired"); }
  135 |       if (!mappingMatches(row)) { update(row, "rejected"); db.exec("COMMIT"); return result(row, "changed" ); }
  136 |       const lock = db.prepare("SELECT request_id FROM calendar_booking_locks WHERE calendar_id=?").get(row.calendar_id);
  137 |       if (lock) { db.exec("COMMIT"); return result(row, "busy"); }
  138 |       db.prepare("INSERT INTO calendar_booking_locks VALUES (?,?,?)").run(row.calendar_id, row.request_id, now().toISO());
  139 |       update(row, "pending");
  140 |       db.exec("COMMIT");
  141 |       return { ...result(row), claimed: true };
  142 |     } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
  143 |   }

EXPLANATION
Within one database transaction, checks the request's state, expiry and current
calendar. If another request owns the calendar lock, reports busy. Otherwise saves the
lock and pending state before external work.

--- F02-B10: Check other confirmed app bookings ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 144-166
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L144-L166

CODE
  144 |   async function confirmedConflict(row, api, bufferMinutes) {
  145 |     const others = db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM booking_requests WHERE calendar_id=? AND request_id<>? AND state='confirmed'
  146 |       AND start_ms<? AND end_ms>?`).all(row.calendar_id, row.request_id,
  147 |       row.end_ms + bufferMinutes * 60000, row.start_ms - bufferMinutes * 60000);
  148 |     for (const other of others) {
  149 |       let event;
  150 |       try { event = (await api.events.get({ calendarId: other.calendar_id, eventId: other.event_id })).data; }
  151 |       catch (error) {
  152 |         if (Number(error.response?.status || error.code) !== 404) throw error;
  153 |         update(other, "removed");
  154 |         continue;
  155 |       }
  156 |       if (event.status === "cancelled") { update(other, "removed"); continue; }
  157 |       if (!owns(event, other)) throw new Error("Stored appointment ownership could not be verified");
  158 |       const start = DateTime.fromISO(event.start?.dateTime || "").toMillis();
  159 |       const end = DateTime.fromISO(event.end?.dateTime || "").toMillis();
  160 |       if (!Number.isFinite(start) || !Number.isFinite(end) || end <= start) throw new Error("Stored appointment time could not be verified");
  161 |       db.prepare("UPDATE booking_requests SET slot_start=?,slot_end=?,start_ms=?,end_ms=?,updated_at=? WHERE request_id=?")
  162 |         .run(event.start.dateTime, event.end.dateTime, start, end, now().toISO(), other.request_id);
  163 |       if (row.start_ms < end + bufferMinutes * 60000 && row.end_ms + bufferMinutes * 60000 > start) return true;
  164 |     }
  165 |     return false;
  166 |   }

EXPLANATION
Rereads relevant known events and honors their real current times, including buffers.
Missing/cancelled events can update local status. This adds protection if FreeBusy
visibility lags; it cannot make Google's separate availability check and write atomic.

--- F02-B11: Confirm, create the owner-visible event, and verify the result ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 167-216
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L167-L216

CODE
  167 |   async function confirm(context) {
  168 |     if (!enabled) return { status: "disabled" };
  169 |     let row = lookup(context);
  170 |     if (!row) return { status: "invalid" };
  171 |     if (row.state === "confirmed") return result(row);
  172 |     if (["pending", "uncertain"].includes(row.state)) return reconcile(context);
  173 |     await authorize();
  174 |     const decision = claim(context);
  175 |     if (!decision.claimed) return decision;
  176 |     row = lookup(context);
  177 |     let attempted = false;
  178 |     try {
  179 |       const customer = db.prepare("SELECT customer_id,whatsapp_id FROM business_customers WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?").get(row.business_id, row.sender_whatsapp_id);
  180 |       if (customer?.customer_id !== row.customer_id) return terminal(row, "rejected");
  181 |       const available = await availability.listSlots(row.business_id, selectedDate({ start: row.slot_start }, availability.configuration(row.business_id).settings.timeZone));
  182 |       const valid = available.some(slot => DateTime.fromISO(slot.start).toMillis() === row.start_ms && DateTime.fromISO(slot.end).toMillis() === row.end_ms);
  183 |       if (!valid || !mappingMatches(row)) return terminal(row, "rejected");
  184 |       const { settings } = availability.configuration(row.business_id);
  185 |       const api = await calendar();
  186 |       if (await confirmedConflict(row, api, settings.bufferMinutes)) return terminal(row, "rejected");
  187 |       if (!mappingMatches(row)) return terminal(row, "rejected");
  188 |       // Display the existing scoped identity internationally; keep the original
  189 |       // Meta identifier in the journal and private ownership metadata.
  190 |       const whatsappNumber = customer.whatsapp_id.replace(/^\+/, "").replace(/^521(?=\d{10}$)/, "52");
  191 |       const requestBody = {
  192 |         id: row.event_id, summary: `${row.customer_name} — Cita`,
  193 |         description: [
  194 |           `Cliente: ${row.customer_name}`, `WhatsApp: +${whatsappNumber}`,
  195 |           "Estado: Reservada", "Origen: NukMiris WhatsApp",
  196 |         ].join("\n"),
  197 |         start: { dateTime: row.slot_start, timeZone: settings.timeZone },
  198 |         end: { dateTime: row.slot_end, timeZone: settings.timeZone },
  199 |         transparency: "opaque",
  200 |         extendedProperties: { private: {
  201 |           nukmirisBusinessId: row.business_id, nukmirisCustomerId: row.sender_whatsapp_id,
  202 |           nukmirisInternalCustomerId: row.customer_id, nukmirisBookingRequestId: row.request_id, nukmirisPOC: "true",
  203 |         } },
  204 |       };
  205 |       attempted = true;
  206 |       const response = await api.events.insert({ calendarId: row.calendar_id, requestBody, sendUpdates: "none" }, { retry: false });
  207 |       if (!matches(response.data, row)) throw new Error("Calendar response did not verify the requested appointment");
  208 |       return terminal(row, "confirmed");
  209 |     } catch (error) {
  210 |       const code = Number(error.response?.status || error.code);
  211 |       if (!attempted || [400, 401, 403, 404, 410, 422].includes(code)) return terminal(row, "rejected");
  212 |       db.prepare("UPDATE booking_requests SET state='uncertain',updated_at=? WHERE request_id=? AND state IN ('pending','uncertain')")
  213 |         .run(now().toISO(), row.request_id);
  214 |       return reconcile(context);
  215 |     }
  216 |   }

EXPLANATION
Known outcomes are reused; uncertain work is reconciled rather than reinserted. A fresh
request rechecks customer identity, availability, calendar mapping and conflicts. Lines
189-204 build the full-name title, international WhatsApp description, start/end and
unchanged private ownership fields. No service placeholder is added. Definite rejection
ends safely; an unclear write response stays protected for verification.

--- F02-B12: Decline and verify service readiness ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 217-234
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking.js#L217-L234

CODE
  217 |   function decline(context) {
  218 |     if (!enabled) return { status: "disabled" };
  219 |     db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
  220 |     try {
  221 |       const row = lookup(context);
  222 |       if (!row) { db.exec("COMMIT"); return { status: "invalid" }; }
  223 |       if (row.state === "offered") update(row, "declined");
  224 |       db.exec("COMMIT");
  225 |       return result(row, ["pending", "uncertain"].includes(row.state) ? "uncertain" : row.state);
  226 |     } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
  227 |   }
  228 |   async function verify() {
  229 |     if (!enabled) throw new Error("Automatic booking is disabled");
  230 |     await authorize();
  231 |   }
  232 |   return { createIntent, confirm, decline, reconcile, invalidateOffers, verify };
  233 | }
  234 |

EXPLANATION
Declining affects only an unused offered request. verify checks event-write
authorization. The returned methods are called by the main program; a function named
confirm here executes the already selected booking, not an extra WhatsApp confirmation
screen.

    8 | function prepareManagementSchema(db) {
    9 |   db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON; BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
   10 |   try {
   11 |     db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS appointment_changes (
   12 |       request_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   13 |       booking_request_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES booking_requests(request_id),
   14 |       business_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   15 |       sender_whatsapp_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   16 |       customer_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   17 |       calendar_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   18 |       event_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   19 |       kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(kind IN ('reschedule','cancel')),
   20 |       original_start TEXT NOT NULL,
   21 |       original_end TEXT NOT NULL,
   22 |       original_etag TEXT NOT NULL,
   23 |       target_start TEXT,
   24 |       target_end TEXT,
   25 |       state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('offered','pending','uncertain','applied','rejected','declined')),
   26 |       expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   27 |       created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   28 |       updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   29 |       FOREIGN KEY(business_id,customer_id) REFERENCES business_customers(business_id,customer_id)
   30 |     );
   31 |     CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_appointment_changes_customer
   32 |       ON appointment_changes(business_id,sender_whatsapp_id,state);`);
   33 |     db.exec("COMMIT");
   34 |   } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
   35 | }
   36 |

EXPLANATION
