--- F03-B03: Create service and verify scoped request relationships ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 37-60
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L37-L60

CODE
   37 | function createAppointmentManagement({ db, availability, enabled = false, lookaheadDays,
   38 |   getClient = () => loadCalendarClient({ booking: true }), verifyAuthorization = verifyBookingAuthorization,
   39 |   now = () => DateTime.now(), lifetimeMs = 15 * 60 * 1000 }) {
   40 |   let client, authorization;
   41 |   async function calendar() { return client || (client = await getClient()); }
   42 |   function authorize() {
   43 |     if (!authorization) authorization = Promise.resolve().then(verifyAuthorization).catch(error => { authorization = undefined; throw error; });
   44 |     return authorization;
   45 |   }
   46 |   function requireEnabled() { if (!enabled) throw new Error("Appointment management is disabled"); }
   47 |   function booking(context) {
   48 |     return db.prepare(`SELECT b.* FROM booking_requests b JOIN business_customers c
   49 |       ON c.business_id=b.business_id AND c.customer_id=b.customer_id AND c.whatsapp_id=b.sender_whatsapp_id
   50 |       WHERE b.request_id=? AND b.business_id=? AND b.sender_whatsapp_id=?`)
   51 |       .get(context.bookingRequestId, context.businessId, context.sender);
   52 |   }
   53 |   function lookup(context) {
   54 |     return db.prepare(`SELECT a.* FROM appointment_changes a JOIN booking_requests b
   55 |       ON b.request_id=a.booking_request_id AND b.business_id=a.business_id
   56 |       AND b.sender_whatsapp_id=a.sender_whatsapp_id AND b.customer_id=a.customer_id
   57 |       AND b.calendar_id=a.calendar_id AND b.event_id=a.event_id
   58 |       WHERE a.request_id=? AND a.business_id=? AND a.sender_whatsapp_id=?`)
   59 |       .get(context.requestId, context.businessId, context.sender);
   60 |   }

EXPLANATION
Initializes the Calendar connection and authorization. Booking/change queries join the
saved customer, business, sender, calendar and event identities. A change request cannot
be used as an unrelated customer's operation.

--- F03-B04: Ownership and result helpers ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 61-77
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L61-L77

CODE
   61 |   function mappingMatches(row) { return availability.configuration(row.business_id).calendarId === row.calendar_id; }
   62 |   function owns(event, row) {
   63 |     const fields = event?.extendedProperties?.private;
   64 |     return event?.id === row.event_id && fields?.nukmirisBusinessId === row.business_id &&
   65 |       fields?.nukmirisCustomerId === row.sender_whatsapp_id && fields?.nukmirisInternalCustomerId === row.customer_id &&
   66 |       fields?.nukmirisBookingRequestId === (row.booking_request_id || row.request_id);
   67 |   }
   68 |   function sameTime(actual, expected) { return DateTime.fromISO(actual || "").toMillis() === DateTime.fromISO(expected || "").toMillis(); }
   69 |   function describe(row) {
   70 |     return { status: row.state, requestId: row.request_id, bookingRequestId: row.booking_request_id,
   71 |       kind: row.kind, originalStart: row.original_start, originalEnd: row.original_end,
   72 |       start: row.target_start, end: row.target_end, eventId: row.event_id };
   73 |   }
   74 |   function changeState(row, state) {
   75 |     db.prepare("UPDATE appointment_changes SET state=?,updated_at=? WHERE request_id=?").run(state, now().toISO(), row.request_id);
   76 |     row.state = state;
   77 |   }

EXPLANATION
Checks the exact calendar and private event ownership metadata, compares times,
describes outcomes and updates saved change state. These are checks on records, not
customer-facing name matching.

--- F03-B05: Read one manageable appointment ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 78-90
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L78-L90

CODE
   78 |   async function readAppointment(context) {
   79 |     requireEnabled();
   80 |     const row = booking(context);
   81 |     if (!row || row.state !== "confirmed" || !mappingMatches(row)) throw new Error("Appointment does not belong to this customer/calendar");
   82 |     const event = (await (await calendar()).events.get({ calendarId: row.calendar_id, eventId: row.event_id })).data;
   83 |     const start = DateTime.fromISO(event?.start?.dateTime || ""), end = DateTime.fromISO(event?.end?.dateTime || "");
   84 |     if (event?.status === "cancelled") throw Object.assign(new Error("Appointment changed or is no longer manageable"), { code: "APPOINTMENT_GONE" });
   85 |     if (!owns(event, row) || !event.etag || event.recurringEventId || event.recurrence ||
   86 |         !start.isValid || !end.isValid || start <= now() || end <= start ||
   87 |         (context.expectedEtag && event.etag !== context.expectedEtag)) throw new Error("Appointment changed or is no longer manageable");
   88 |     return { row, event, bookingRequestId: row.request_id, start: event.start.dateTime,
   89 |       end: event.end.dateTime, etag: event.etag, calendarId: row.calendar_id };
   90 |   }

EXPLANATION
Requires an owned confirmed booking, matching calendar, existing future event, valid
start/end, version stamp and nonrecurring event. A supplied expected version must still
match, preventing a stale selection from silently changing a newer source event.

--- F03-B06: List this customer's valid upcoming appointments ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 91-108
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L91-L108

CODE
   91 |   async function listAppointments({ businessId, sender }) {
   92 |     requireEnabled();
   93 |     const { calendarId } = availability.configuration(businessId);
   94 |     const rows = db.prepare(`SELECT request_id FROM booking_requests WHERE business_id=? AND sender_whatsapp_id=?
   95 |       AND calendar_id=? AND state='confirmed' ORDER BY start_ms`).all(businessId, sender, calendarId);
   96 |     const appointments = [];
   97 |     for (const row of rows) {
   98 |       // Invalid/missing ownership is omitted. Service/network failures must not
   99 |       // turn into a misleading empty appointment list.
  100 |       try { appointments.push(await readAppointment({ businessId, sender, bookingRequestId: row.request_id })); }
  101 |       catch (error) {
  102 |         const code = Number(error.response?.status || error.code);
  103 |         if (![404, 410].includes(code) && !/Appointment (does not belong|changed)/.test(error.message)) throw error;
  104 |       }
  105 |     }
  106 |     return appointments.sort((a, b) => DateTime.fromISO(a.start).toMillis() - DateTime.fromISO(b.start).toMillis())
  107 |       .map(({ row, event, ...item }) => item);
  108 |   }

EXPLANATION
Starts from this business/customer's saved confirmed bookings and rereads Google for
each. Missing, cancelled, past or invalidly owned entries are omitted. A service failure
is propagated instead of being mistaken for an empty list.

--- F03-B07: Find replacement times while excluding the original event ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 109-140
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L109-L140

CODE
  109 |   async function listSlots(context, query = {}) {
  110 |     const source = await readAppointment(context);
  111 |     const { settings, searchPolicy } = availability.configuration(context.businessId);
  112 |     const policy = validateSearchPolicy({ ...(searchPolicy || readSearchPolicy()),
  113 |       ...(lookaheadDays === undefined ? {} : { maxBookingHorizonDays: lookaheadDays }) });
  114 |     const api = await calendar();
  115 |     return searchAvailableSlots({ settings, now: now(), policy, query, excludeSlot: { start: source.start, end: source.end },
  116 |       readBusy: async ({ start, end }) => {
  117 |         const periods = [], seen = new Set();
  118 |         let pageToken;
  119 |         do {
  120 |           const response = await api.events.list({ calendarId: source.calendarId, singleEvents: true, showDeleted: false,
  121 |             timeMin: start.toISO(), timeMax: end.toISO(), timeZone: settings.timeZone,
  122 |             maxResults: 2500, ...(pageToken ? { pageToken } : {}) }, { timeout: 15000, retry: false });
  123 |           const items = response.data.items || [];
  124 |           if (!Array.isArray(items)) throw new Error("Calendar events could not be checked");
  125 |           for (const event of items) {
  126 |             if (event.id === source.row.event_id || event.status === "cancelled" || event.transparency === "transparent") continue;
  127 |             const first = DateTime.fromISO(event.start?.dateTime || event.start?.date || "", { zone: settings.timeZone });
  128 |             const last = DateTime.fromISO(event.end?.dateTime || event.end?.date || "", { zone: settings.timeZone });
  129 |             if (!first.isValid || !last.isValid || last <= first) throw new Error("Calendar returned an invalid busy event");
  130 |             periods.push({ start: first.toISO(), end: last.toISO() });
  131 |           }
  132 |           pageToken = response.data.nextPageToken;
  133 |           if (pageToken && seen.has(pageToken)) throw new Error("Calendar returned repeated pagination");
  134 |           if (pageToken) seen.add(pageToken);
  135 |         } while (pageToken);
  136 |         return periods;
  137 |       },
  138 |     });
  139 |   }
  140 |

EXPLANATION
Runs the shared schedule/search engine, but reads Calendar events in pages so the
appointment being moved can be excluded from busy time. Other opaque/all-day events
still block time. It rejects malformed busy events or repeated page tokens.

--- F03-B08: Create a local change request ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 141-167
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L141-L167

CODE
  141 |   function invalidateOffers({ businessId, sender }) {
  142 |     db.prepare("UPDATE appointment_changes SET state='declined',updated_at=? WHERE business_id=? AND sender_whatsapp_id=? AND state='offered'")
  143 |       .run(now().toISO(), businessId, sender);
  144 |   }
  145 |   async function createIntent({ businessId, sender, bookingRequestId, expectedEtag, kind, slot }) {
  146 |     requireEnabled();
  147 |     if (!["reschedule", "cancel"].includes(kind)) throw new Error("Invalid appointment action");
  148 |     const context = { businessId, sender, bookingRequestId, expectedEtag };
  149 |     const source = await readAppointment(context);
  150 |     if (kind === "reschedule") {
  151 |       if (!slot || !(await listSlots(context, selectedDate(slot, availability.configuration(businessId).settings.timeZone))).some(item => sameTime(item.start, slot.start) && sameTime(item.end, slot.end))) {
  152 |         throw Object.assign(new Error("The selected new time is unavailable"), { code: "SLOT_UNAVAILABLE" });
  153 |       }
  154 |     }
  155 |     const requestId = randomUUID();
  156 |     db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
  157 |     try {
  158 |       invalidateOffers({ businessId, sender });
  159 |       db.prepare(`INSERT INTO appointment_changes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'offered',?,?,?)`)
  160 |         .run(requestId, bookingRequestId, businessId, sender, source.row.customer_id, source.calendarId,
  161 |           source.row.event_id, kind, source.start, source.end, source.etag,
  162 |           kind === "reschedule" ? slot.start : null, kind === "reschedule" ? slot.end : null,
  163 |           now().toMillis() + lifetimeMs, now().toISO(), now().toISO());
  164 |       db.exec("COMMIT");
  165 |     } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
  166 |     return describe(lookup({ requestId, businessId, sender }));
  167 |   }

EXPLANATION
Declines earlier unused changes for this sender, rereads the source and, for a move,
verifies the target slot. Saves original identity/version and target details. No event
is moved or deleted by merely saving this intent.

--- F03-B09: Claim a change under the shared calendar lock ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 168-187
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L168-L187

CODE
  168 |   function claim(context) {
  169 |     db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
  170 |     try {
  171 |       const row = lookup(context);
  172 |       if (!row) { db.exec("COMMIT"); return { status: "invalid" }; }
  173 |       if (row.state !== "offered") { db.exec("COMMIT"); return describe(row); }
  174 |       if (row.expires_at < now().toMillis() || !mappingMatches(row) ||
  175 |           booking({ ...context, bookingRequestId: row.booking_request_id })?.state !== "confirmed") {
  176 |         changeState(row, "rejected"); db.exec("COMMIT"); return describe(row);
  177 |       }
  178 |       if (db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM calendar_booking_locks WHERE calendar_id=?").get(row.calendar_id)) {
  179 |         db.exec("COMMIT"); return { ...describe(row), status: "busy" };
  180 |       }
  181 |       // Share the existing calendar lock with new bookings. The parent booking
  182 |       // is its FK owner; the change journal identifies the operation itself.
  183 |       db.prepare("INSERT INTO calendar_booking_locks VALUES (?,?,?)").run(row.calendar_id, row.booking_request_id, now().toISO());
  184 |       changeState(row, "pending");
  185 |       db.exec("COMMIT"); return { ...describe(row), claimed: true };
  186 |     } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
  187 |   }

EXPLANATION
Checks expiry, state, mapping and source, then acquires the same persistent calendar
lock family used for bookings. This prevents participating app requests from writing
through one another.

--- F03-B10: Finish a change without overwriting newer work ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 188-205
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L188-L205

CODE
  188 |   function terminal(context, state) {
  189 |     db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
  190 |     try {
  191 |       const row = lookup(context);
  192 |       // A late duplicate recovery cannot release a newer operation's lock.
  193 |       if (!row || !["pending", "uncertain"].includes(row.state)) {
  194 |         db.exec("COMMIT"); return row ? describe(row) : { status: "invalid" };
  195 |       }
  196 |       changeState(row, state);
  197 |       if (state === "applied") {
  198 |         if (row.kind === "cancel") db.prepare("UPDATE booking_requests SET state='removed',updated_at=? WHERE request_id=?").run(now().toISO(), row.booking_request_id);
  199 |         else db.prepare("UPDATE booking_requests SET slot_start=?,slot_end=?,start_ms=?,end_ms=?,updated_at=? WHERE request_id=?")
  200 |           .run(row.target_start, row.target_end, DateTime.fromISO(row.target_start).toMillis(), DateTime.fromISO(row.target_end).toMillis(), now().toISO(), row.booking_request_id);
  201 |       }
  202 |       db.prepare("DELETE FROM calendar_booking_locks WHERE calendar_id=? AND request_id=?").run(row.calendar_id, row.booking_request_id);
  203 |       db.exec("COMMIT"); return describe(row);
  204 |     } catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
  205 |   }

EXPLANATION
Rereads the change atomically. Applied cancellation marks the source booking removed;
applied movement updates its saved times. The matching source lock is released only for
the operation being completed.

--- F03-B11: Verify or reconcile a possibly completed change ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 206-224
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L206-L224

CODE
  206 |   function applied(event, row) {
  207 |     return owns(event, row) && event.status !== "cancelled" &&
  208 |       event.extendedProperties.private.nukmirisChangeRequestId === row.request_id &&
  209 |       sameTime(event.start?.dateTime, row.target_start) && sameTime(event.end?.dateTime, row.target_end);
  210 |   }
  211 |   async function reconcile(context) {
  212 |     if (!enabled) return { status: "disabled" };
  213 |     const row = lookup(context);
  214 |     if (!row) return { status: "invalid" };
  215 |     if (!["pending", "uncertain"].includes(row.state)) return describe(row);
  216 |     if (!mappingMatches(row)) return { ...describe(row), status: "uncertain" };
  217 |     try {
  218 |       const event = (await (await calendar()).events.get({ calendarId: row.calendar_id, eventId: row.event_id })).data;
  219 |       if (row.kind === "cancel" ? event.id === row.event_id && event.status === "cancelled" : applied(event, row)) return terminal(context, "applied");
  220 |     } catch (error) {
  221 |       if (row.kind === "cancel" && [404, 410].includes(Number(error.response?.status || error.code))) return terminal(context, "applied");
  222 |     }
  223 |     return { ...describe(row), status: "uncertain" };
  224 |   }

EXPLANATION
A move must match ownership, change-request ID and target times. Cancellation can be
verified by a missing/cancelled event. Unknown results remain uncertain and keep
protection; the code does not blindly repeat the patch or delete.

--- F03-B12: Check journal conflicts before moving ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 225-242
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L225-L242

CODE
  225 |   async function journalConflict(row, api) {
  226 |     const { settings } = availability.configuration(row.business_id);
  227 |     const buffer = settings.bufferMinutes * 60000;
  228 |     const start = DateTime.fromISO(row.target_start).toMillis(), end = DateTime.fromISO(row.target_end).toMillis();
  229 |     const others = db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM booking_requests WHERE calendar_id=? AND request_id<>? AND state='confirmed'
  230 |       AND start_ms<? AND end_ms>?`).all(row.calendar_id, row.booking_request_id, end + buffer, start - buffer);
  231 |     for (const other of others) {
  232 |       let event;
  233 |       try { event = (await api.events.get({ calendarId: other.calendar_id, eventId: other.event_id })).data; }
  234 |       catch (error) { if ([404, 410].includes(Number(error.response?.status || error.code))) continue; throw error; }
  235 |       if (event.status === "cancelled") continue;
  236 |       if (!owns(event, other)) throw new Error("Other appointment ownership could not be verified");
  237 |       const first = DateTime.fromISO(event.start?.dateTime || "").toMillis(), last = DateTime.fromISO(event.end?.dateTime || "").toMillis();
  238 |       if (!Number.isFinite(first) || !Number.isFinite(last) || last <= first) throw new Error("Other appointment time is invalid");
  239 |       if (start < last + buffer && end + buffer > first) return true;
  240 |     }
  241 |     return false;
  242 |   }

EXPLANATION
Compares the target against other confirmed app appointments and their current Calendar
state. Handles missing/changed event times while preserving ownership checks and
configured buffer rules.

--- F03-B13: Apply the selected cancellation or reschedule ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-management.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 243-286
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-management.js#L243-L286

CODE
  243 |   async function confirm(context) {
  244 |     if (!enabled) return { status: "disabled" };
  245 |     let row = lookup(context);
  246 |     if (!row) return { status: "invalid" };
  247 |     if (["pending", "uncertain"].includes(row.state)) return reconcile(context);
  248 |     if (row.state !== "offered") return describe(row);
  249 |     await authorize();
  250 |     const decision = claim(context);
  251 |     if (!decision.claimed) return decision;
  252 |     row = lookup(context);
  253 |     let attempted = false;
  254 |     try {
  255 |       const sourceContext = { businessId: row.business_id, sender: row.sender_whatsapp_id,
  256 |         bookingRequestId: row.booking_request_id, expectedEtag: row.original_etag };
  257 |       const source = await readAppointment(sourceContext);
  258 |       if (!sameTime(source.start, row.original_start) || !sameTime(source.end, row.original_end)) return terminal(context, "rejected");
  259 |       const api = await calendar();
  260 |       if (row.kind === "reschedule") {
  261 |         const slots = await listSlots(sourceContext, selectedDate({ start: row.target_start }, availability.configuration(row.business_id).settings.timeZone));
  262 |         if (!slots.some(slot => sameTime(slot.start, row.target_start) && sameTime(slot.end, row.target_end)) ||
  263 |             await journalConflict(row, api)) return terminal(context, "rejected");
  264 |       }
  265 |       if (!mappingMatches(row)) return terminal(context, "rejected");
  266 |       const options = { retry: false, headers: { "If-Match": row.original_etag } };
  267 |       attempted = true;
  268 |       if (row.kind === "cancel") {
  269 |         await api.events.delete({ calendarId: row.calendar_id, eventId: row.event_id, sendUpdates: "none" }, options);
  270 |         return terminal(context, "applied");
  271 |       }
  272 |       const { settings } = availability.configuration(row.business_id);
  273 |       const response = await api.events.patch({ calendarId: row.calendar_id, eventId: row.event_id, sendUpdates: "none", requestBody: {
  274 |         start: { dateTime: row.target_start, timeZone: settings.timeZone },
  275 |         end: { dateTime: row.target_end, timeZone: settings.timeZone },
  276 |         extendedProperties: { private: { ...source.event.extendedProperties.private, nukmirisChangeRequestId: row.request_id } },
  277 |       } }, options);
  278 |       if (!applied(response.data, row)) throw new Error("Reschedule response could not be verified");
  279 |       return terminal(context, "applied");
  280 |     } catch (error) {
  281 |       if (!attempted || [400, 401, 403, 404, 410, 412, 422].includes(Number(error.response?.status || error.code))) return terminal(context, "rejected");
  282 |       db.prepare("UPDATE appointment_changes SET state='uncertain',updated_at=? WHERE request_id=? AND state IN ('pending','uncertain')")
  283 |         .run(now().toISO(), row.request_id);
  284 |       return reconcile(context);
  285 |     }
  286 |   }

EXPLANATION
Reuses earlier results, checks the source again and validates replacement availability.
Cancellation deletes only the owned event. Rescheduling patches only times and private
change metadata, retaining title and description. Both use the original version stamp;
definite failures are rejected and unclear writes are reconciled.

EXPLANATION

   58 |   }
   59 |

--- F04-B04: Date lists and later date pages ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability-menu.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 60-89
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability-menu.js#L60-L89

CODE
   60 |   async function dates(from, session, page = 0) {
   61 |     beginView(session, "dates");
   62 |     const { zone, policy, horizonDays } = context(from);
   63 |     session.datePages ||= [session.slots];
   64 |     if (!session.datePages[page]) {
   65 |       const previous = session.datePages[page - 1];
   66 |       if (!previous?.length) throw new Error("Invalid date page");
   67 |       const afterDate = local(previous.at(-1).start, zone).toISODate();
   68 |       session.datePages[page] = await availability.listSlots(businessFor(from).business_id, { afterDate });
   69 |     }
   70 |     session.slots = session.datePages[page];
   71 |     const days = [...new Set(session.slots.map(slot => local(slot.start, zone).toISODate()))].slice(0, policy.targetDates);
   72 |     const rows = days.map(day => ({
   73 |       id: action(session, "date", day),
   74 |       title: local(day, zone).toFormat("ccc d 'de' LLLL"),
   75 |       description: "Ver horarios de este día",
   76 |     }));
   77 |     if (page > 0) rows.push({ id: action(session, "days", page - 1), title: "Fechas anteriores" });
   78 |     const lastDay = days.at(-1);
   79 |     if (lastDay && days.length >= policy.targetDates && local(lastDay, zone).plus({ days: 1 }) < now().setZone(zone).plus({ days: horizonDays })) {
   80 |       rows.push({ id: action(session, "days", page + 1), title: "Más fechas" });
   81 |     }
   82 |     save(from, session, rows);
   83 |     if (!rows.length) {
   84 |       await sendMessage(from, "No encontré horarios disponibles en los próximos días.\n\n" + datePrompt(from));
   85 |       return;
   86 |     }
   87 |     await sendAppointmentList(from, (days.length ? "" : "No encontré más fechas disponibles.\n\n") + datePrompt(from), rows, "Elegir fecha", "Fechas disponibles");
   88 |   }
   89 |

EXPLANATION
Offers available dates with navigation within WhatsApp's row limit. Later pages start
after the previous page's last date. The helper keeps cached page snapshots but performs
a fresh check when the customer selects an actual date.

--- F04-B07: Check a selected or parsed date ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability-menu.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 117-142
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability-menu.js#L117-L142

CODE
  117 |   async function chooseDate(from, session, day, fallback) {
  118 |     const { zone, horizonDays } = context(from);
  119 |     const current = now().setZone(zone);
  120 |     const today = current.startOf("day");
  121 |     const selected = DateTime.fromISO(day, { zone });
  122 |     if (!selected.isValid || selected.toISODate() !== day || selected < today || selected >= current.plus({ days: horizonDays })) {
  123 |       const error = !selected.isValid || selected.toISODate() !== day ? "INVALID" : selected < today ? "PAST" : "OUT_OF_HORIZON";
  124 |       await sendMessage(from, dateErrorMessage({ error }, horizonDays));
  125 |       return;
  126 |     }
  127 |     // Recheck Google after the customer picks a day; never reuse cached times
  128 |     // to claim a newly busy appointment is still available.
  129 |     let fresh = await availability.listSlots(businessFor(from).business_id, { date: day });
  130 |     if (!fresh.length && fallback) {
  131 |       if (!fallback.success) {
  132 |         await sendMessage(from, dateErrorMessage(fallback, horizonDays));
  133 |         return;
  134 |       }
  135 |       day = fallback.date;
  136 |       fresh = await availability.listSlots(businessFor(from).business_id, { date: day });
  137 |     }
  138 |     session.slots = fresh.filter(slot => local(slot.start, zone).toISODate() === day);
  139 |     session.selectedDate = day;
  140 |     await times(from, session);
  141 |   }
  142 |

EXPLANATION
Rejects an invalid, past or out-of-horizon date. Reads real availability directly for
that day. A bare weekday meaning today can try next week only if today has no valid
times and the fallback is allowed; lookup failures do not count as an empty day.

  155 |   async function handleSelection(from, command) {
  156 |     const session = sessions.get(from);
  157 |     if (!valid(from, session) || !session.actions?.includes(command)) {
  158 |       await sendMessage(from, expired);
  159 |       return;
  160 |     }
  161 |     const [, , kind, value] = command.split(":");

  166 |     if (kind === "times") return times(from, session, Number(value));

--- F05-B04: Existing-appointment choices and pagination ---
FILE: backend/lib/management-menu.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 33-49
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/management-menu.js#L33-L49

   33 |   async function choices(from, session, page = 0) {
   34 |     session.selectionId = randomUUID();
   35 |     const visible = session.appointments.slice(page * 8, (page + 1) * 8);
   36 |     const rows = visible.map((item, index) => ({
   37 |       id: `owned:${session.selectionId}:pick:${page * 8 + index}`,
   38 |       title: format(from, item.start, true), description: `Termina: ${format(from, item.end, true)}`,
   39 |     }));
   40 |     if (page > 0) rows.push({ id: `owned:${session.selectionId}:page:${page - 1}`, title: "Citas anteriores" });
   41 |     if ((page + 1) * 8 < session.appointments.length) rows.push({ id: `owned:${session.selectionId}:page:${page + 1}`, title: "Más citas" });
   42 |     session.actions = rows.map(row => row.id);
   43 |     sessions.set(from, session);
   44 |     const body = session.kind === "cancel"
   45 |       ? "Selecciona la cita que deseas cancelar. Se cancelará al seleccionarla."
   46 |       : "Selecciona la cita que deseas reagendar.";
   47 |     if (rows.length <= 3) await sendReplyButtons(from, body, rows);
   48 |     else await sendAppointmentList(from, body, rows);
   49 |   }

EXPLANATION
Builds owned-action IDs tied to a fresh selection identity and the original appointment
indexes. Up to eight appointments appear per page, with navigation as needed; up to
three rows use buttons, larger pages use a list. Cancellation copy tells the customer
that selecting the appointment will cancel it.

--- F05-B06: Remember the reschedule source and reuse availability browsing ---
FILE: backend/lib/management-menu.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 61-85
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/management-menu.js#L61-L85

   61 |   async function beginReschedule(from, appointment, notice) {
   62 |     const scope = { ...context(from), bookingRequestId: appointment.bookingRequestId, expectedEtag: appointment.etag };
   63 |     await management.readAppointment(scope);
   64 |     sessions.set(from, { mode: "reschedule_browse", bookingRequestId: appointment.bookingRequestId,
   65 |       expectedEtag: appointment.etag, expiresAt: Date.now() + lifetime });
   66 |     await rescheduleFlow(from).offer(from, { notice });
   67 |   }
   68 |   function sourceContext(from) {
   69 |     const session = sessions.get(from);
   70 |     if (!active(session, "reschedule_browse")) throw new Error("Expired rescheduling session");
   71 |     return { ...context(from), bookingRequestId: session.bookingRequestId, expectedEtag: session.expectedEtag };
   72 |   }
   73 |   function rescheduleFlow(from) {
   74 |     return createAvailabilityMenu({

   76 |         listSlots: (businessId, query) => management.listSlots(sourceContext(from), query) },
   77 |       sessions: {
   78 |         get: () => sessions.get(from)?.mode === "reschedule_browse" ? sessions.get(from).browse : undefined,
   79 |         set: (to, browse) => sessions.set(from, { ...sessions.get(from), browse }),
   80 |         delete: () => sessions.delete(from),
   81 |       },
   82 |       businessFor, sendMessage, sendReplyButtons, sendAppointmentList, bookingEnabled: true, lookaheadDays, lifetime,
   83 |       onSelect: (to, slot, session, command) => applySelection(to, { ...sourceContext(to), kind: "reschedule", slot }, command),
   84 |     });
   85 |   }

EXPLANATION
Rereads the selected appointment and keeps its request ID/version with a nested browsing

--- F05-B07: Save a selected change before execution ---

   86 |   async function applySelection(from, details, selectionCommand) {

   98 |       }
   99 |       throw error;
  100 |     }

--- F05-B08: Execute or check a change and send its result ---
FILE: backend/lib/management-menu.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 104-133
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/management-menu.js#L104-L133

CODE
  104 |   async function decide(from, requestId, decision) {
  105 |     beforeDecision(from, requestId, decision);
  106 |     const scope = { ...context(from), requestId };
  107 |     const outcome = decision === "status" ? await management.reconcile(scope)
  108 |       : decision === "no" ? management.decline(scope) : await management.confirm(scope);
  109 |     if (outcome.status === "busy") {
  110 |       await sendReplyButtons(from, "Estamos comprobando otra solicitud. Intenta de nuevo en unos momentos.",
  111 |         [{ id: `change:${requestId}:retry`, title: "Reintentar" }]);
  112 |       return;
  113 |     }
  114 |     if (["pending", "uncertain"].includes(outcome.status)) {
  115 |       console.error("Appointment change needs verification. Request ID:", requestId);
  116 |       await sendReplyButtons(from, "Tu cambio sigue en verificación. Consulta su estado antes de solicitar otro cambio o reserva.",
  117 |         [{ id: `change:${requestId}:status`, title: "Consultar estado" }]);
  118 |       return;
  119 |     }
  120 |     const current = sessions.get(from);
  121 |     if (current?.requestId === requestId && current.mode !== "management_result") sessions.delete(from);
  122 |     if (outcome.status === "applied") {
  123 |       await sendMessage(from, outcome.kind === "cancel"
  124 |         ? `Tu cita del ${range(from, outcome.originalStart, outcome.originalEnd)} en ${businessFor(from).business_name} está cancelada.`
  125 |         : appointmentSuccess({ kind: "reschedule", start: outcome.start, end: outcome.end,
  126 |           zone: availability.configuration(context(from).businessId).settings.timeZone, businessName: businessFor(from).business_name }));
  127 |     } else if (outcome.status === "rejected" && outcome.kind === "reschedule") {
  128 |       const appointment = await management.readAppointment({ ...context(from), bookingRequestId: outcome.bookingRequestId });
  129 |       await beginReschedule(from, appointment, "No pudimos cambiar tu cita a ese horario. Te muestro otras opciones.");
  130 |     } else if (outcome.status === "declined") {
  131 |       await sendMessage(from, "No realizamos cambios en tu cita. " + MENU_INSTRUCTION);
  132 |     } else await sendMessage(from, "No pudimos realizar este cambio. Escribe Reagendar o Cancelar para consultar de nuevo tu cita. " + MENU_INSTRUCTION);
  133 |   }

EXPLANATION
Retries or checks the saved change through the management service. Busy/uncertain work
gets recovery guidance. Applied cancellation or movement gets the existing success
message; rejected movement can show new choices. Messages follow verified outcomes, not
merely button taps.

--- F05-B09: Interactive action routing and stale-choice protection ---
FILE: backend/lib/management-menu.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 134-163
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/management-menu.js#L134-L163

CODE
  134 |   async function handleInteractive(from, command) {
  135 |     const current = sessions.get(from);
  136 |     if (current?.mode === "management_result" && current.selectionCommand === command) {
  137 |       await decide(from, current.requestId, "retry"); return true;
  138 |     }
  139 |     // Previously sent v0.3.0 confirmation buttons remain bound to their journal
  140 |     // request; new flows publish only selections and recovery/status actions.
  141 |     const change = /^change:([0-9a-f-]{36}):(yes|no|retry|status)$/.exec(command);
  142 |     if (change) { await decide(from, change[1], change[2]); return true; }
  143 |     if (command.startsWith("availability:") && sessions.get(from)?.mode === "reschedule_browse") {
  144 |       await rescheduleFlow(from).handleSelection(from, command); return true;
  145 |     }
  146 |     const main = /^menu:([0-9a-f-]{36}):manage$/.exec(command);
  147 |     const manage = /^manage:([0-9a-f-]{36}):(reschedule|cancel)$/.exec(command);
  148 |     const owned = /^owned:([0-9a-f-]{36}):(pick|page):(\d+)$/.exec(command);
  149 |     if (!main && !manage && !owned && !/^(change|owned|manage):/.test(command)) return false;
  150 |     const session = sessions.get(from);
  151 |     if (main && active(session, "main_menu") && session.menuId === main[1]) await showMenu(from);
  152 |     else if (manage && active(session, "management_menu") && session.menuId === manage[1]) await start(from, manage[2]);
  153 |     else if (owned && active(session, "management_select") && session.actions.includes(command)) {
  154 |       if (owned[2] === "page") await choices(from, session, Number(owned[3]));
  155 |       else {
  156 |         const appointment = session.appointments[Number(owned[3])];
  157 |         const scope = { ...context(from), bookingRequestId: appointment.bookingRequestId, expectedEtag: appointment.etag };
  158 |         if (session.kind === "cancel") await applySelection(from, { ...scope, kind: "cancel" }, command);
  159 |         else await beginReschedule(from, appointment);
  160 |       }
  161 |     } else await sendMessage(from, expired);
  162 |     return true;
  163 |   }

EXPLANATION
A repeat of the saved selection checks its original result. Current menus, owned choices
and replacement-time buttons must match their current session. Previously issued
request-specific change/status buttons retain recovery compatibility.

--- F06-B02: Convert HH:mm and validate scheduling settings ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 5-34
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability.js#L5-L34

CODE
    5 | function minutes(value, label) {
    6 |   if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(value)) throw new Error(`${label} must use HH:mm`);
    7 |   const [hour, minute] = value.split(":").map(Number);
    8 |   if (hour > 23 || minute > 59) throw new Error(`${label} is invalid`);
    9 |   return hour * 60 + minute;
   10 | }
   11 |
   12 | function validateSettings(settings) {
   13 |   if (!settings || typeof settings.timeZone !== "string" || !settings.timeZone || !DateTime.now().setZone(settings.timeZone).isValid) throw new Error("A valid business time zone is required");
   14 |   if (!Array.isArray(settings.workingDays) || settings.workingDays.length === 0 ||
   15 |       settings.workingDays.some(day => !Number.isInteger(day) || day < 1 || day > 7)) {
   16 |     throw new Error("Working days must use ISO weekday numbers 1 through 7");
   17 |   }
   18 |   const opening = minutes(settings.openingTime, "Opening time");
   19 |   const closing = minutes(settings.closingTime, "Closing time");
   20 |   if (closing <= opening) throw new Error("Closing time must be after opening time; overnight hours are not supported");
   21 |   for (const [key, minimum] of [["durationMinutes", 1], ["bufferMinutes", 0], ["startIntervalMinutes", 1]]) {
   22 |     if (!Number.isInteger(settings[key]) || settings[key] < minimum || settings[key] > 1440) {
   23 |       throw new Error(`${key} must be an explicitly configured integer between ${minimum} and 1440`);
   24 |     }
   25 |   }
   26 |   if (!Array.isArray(settings.breaks)) throw new Error("Breaks must be explicitly configured; use [] for none");
   27 |   for (const pause of settings.breaks) {
   28 |     const start = minutes(pause.start, "Break start");
   29 |     const end = minutes(pause.end, "Break end");
   30 |     if (start < opening || end > closing || end <= start) throw new Error("Breaks must fit inside business hours");
   31 |   }
   32 |   return settings;
   33 | }
   34 |

EXPLANATION
Checks timezone, workdays, opening/closing time, duration, buffer, start interval and
breaks. Overnight opening hours are not supported. Invalid or missing settings stop the
calculation rather than creating guessed hours.

--- F06-B03: Wall-clock and overlap helpers ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 35-45
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability.js#L35-L45

CODE
   35 | function atMinute(day, minute) {
   36 |   const hour = Math.floor(minute / 60);
   37 |   const value = day.set({ hour, minute: minute % 60, second: 0, millisecond: 0 });
   38 |   // Reject nonexistent wall-clock times instead of shifting a DST booking silently.
   39 |   return value.hour === hour && value.minute === minute % 60 ? value : null;
   40 | }
   41 |
   42 | function overlaps(start, end, blockedStart, blockedEnd) {
   43 |   return start < blockedEnd && end > blockedStart;
   44 | }
   45 |

EXPLANATION
Builds a local time and rejects a nonexistent daylight-saving wall time. Overlap uses
strict boundaries, so adjacent appointments can be allowed when the configured buffer is
zero.

--- F06-B04: Calculate the permitted date range and busy periods ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 46-63
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability.js#L46-L63

CODE
   46 | function findAvailableSlots({ settings, busyPeriods, now = DateTime.now(), lookaheadDays = 14, rangeStart, rangeEnd }) {
   47 |   validateSettings(settings);
   48 |   if (!Number.isInteger(lookaheadDays) || lookaheadDays < 1 || lookaheadDays > 365) throw new Error("Booking horizon must be between 1 and 365 days");
   49 |   if (!Array.isArray(busyPeriods)) throw new Error("Calendar busy periods are required");
   50 |   const current = (typeof now === "string" ? DateTime.fromISO(now) : now).setZone(settings.timeZone);
   51 |   if (!current.isValid) throw new Error("Current time is invalid");
   52 |   const parse = value => (typeof value === "string" ? DateTime.fromISO(value) : value).setZone(settings.timeZone);
   53 |   const first = rangeStart ? parse(rangeStart) : current;
   54 |   const last = rangeEnd ? parse(rangeEnd) : current.plus({ days: lookaheadDays });
   55 |   if (!first.isValid || !last.isValid) throw new Error("Availability range is invalid");
   56 |   const from = DateTime.max(first, current);
   57 |   const until = DateTime.min(last, current.plus({ days: lookaheadDays }));
   58 |   const busy = busyPeriods.map(period => {
   59 |     const start = DateTime.fromISO(period.start, { setZone: true });
   60 |     const end = DateTime.fromISO(period.end, { setZone: true });
   61 |     if (!start.isValid || !end.isValid || end <= start) throw new Error("Calendar returned an invalid busy period");
   62 |     return { start: start.toMillis(), end: end.plus({ minutes: settings.bufferMinutes }).toMillis() };
   63 |   });

EXPLANATION
Uses the business timezone, current time and requested bounds. Google busy intervals
must have valid increasing timestamps. Existing-event buffer time is added to their
blocked ends.

--- F06-B05: Generate and filter every candidate slot ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 64-91
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability.js#L64-L91

CODE
   64 |   const opening = minutes(settings.openingTime, "Opening time");
   65 |   const closing = minutes(settings.closingTime, "Closing time");
   66 |   const slots = [];
   67 |   for (let day = from.startOf("day"); day <= until.startOf("day"); day = day.plus({ days: 1 })) {
   68 |     if (!settings.workingDays.includes(day.weekday)) continue;
   69 |     const close = atMinute(day, closing);
   70 |     if (!close) continue;
   71 |     const breaks = settings.breaks.map(pause => ({
   72 |       start: atMinute(day, minutes(pause.start, "Break start")),
   73 |       end: atMinute(day, minutes(pause.end, "Break end")),
   74 |     }));
   75 |     if (breaks.some(pause => !pause.start || !pause.end)) continue;
   76 |     for (let minute = opening; minute < closing; minute += settings.startIntervalMinutes) {
   77 |       const start = atMinute(day, minute);
   78 |       if (!start || start <= current || start < from || start >= until) continue;
   79 |       const end = start.plus({ minutes: settings.durationMinutes });
   80 |       const occupiedEnd = end.plus({ minutes: settings.bufferMinutes });
   81 |       if (occupiedEnd > close || occupiedEnd > until) continue;
   82 |       const first = start.toMillis();
   83 |       const last = occupiedEnd.toMillis();
   84 |       if (busy.some(period => overlaps(first, last, period.start, period.end))) continue;
   85 |       if (breaks.some(pause => overlaps(first, last, pause.start.toMillis(), pause.end.toMillis()))) continue;
   86 |       slots.push({ start: start.toISO(), end: end.toISO() });
   87 |     }
   88 |   }
   89 |   return slots;
   90 | }
   91 |

EXPLANATION
Walks configured workdays and start intervals. Rejects past starts, lunch/break
overlaps, busy events and appointments/buffers extending past closing or the horizon.
Returns full timestamp pairs, not display labels.

   10 |   targetDates: TARGET_AVAILABLE_DATES, maxBookingHorizonDays: MAX_BOOKING_HORIZON_DAYS });

EXPLANATION

--- F07-B02: Date helpers and allowed boundaries ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability-search.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 26-45
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability-search.js#L26-L45

CODE
   26 | function dayOf(iso, zone) { return DateTime.fromISO(iso).setZone(zone).toISODate(); }
   27 | function selectedDate(slot, zone) { return { date: dayOf(slot.start, zone) }; }
   28 | function rangeError() { return Object.assign(new Error("The requested date is outside the booking horizon"), { code: "DATE_OUT_OF_RANGE" }); }
   29 | function bounds({ settings, now, policy, query = {} }) {
   30 |   const current = (typeof now === "string" ? DateTime.fromISO(now) : now).setZone(settings.timeZone);
   31 |   if (!current.isValid) throw new Error("Current time is invalid");
   32 |   const end = current.plus({ days: policy.maxBookingHorizonDays });
   33 |   function parse(value) {
   34 |     const date = typeof value === "string" && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) ? DateTime.fromISO(value, { zone: settings.timeZone }) : null;
   35 |     if (!date?.isValid || date.toISODate() !== value) throw rangeError();
   36 |     return date;
   37 |   }
   38 |   if (query.date) {
   39 |     const date = parse(query.date);
   40 |     if (date < current.startOf("day") || date >= end) throw rangeError();
   41 |     return { current, start: DateTime.max(date, current), end: DateTime.min(date.plus({ days: 1 }), end), specific: true };
   42 |   }
   43 |   const start = query.afterDate ? DateTime.max(parse(query.afterDate).plus({ days: 1 }), current) : current;
   44 |   return { current, start, end, specific: false };
   45 | }

EXPLANATION
Converts a slot to its business-local day. A requested date must be valid and in range.
The final horizon boundary is current business-local time plus calendar days, so its
last day can be partial.

--- F07-B03: Search progressively without overlapping batches ---
FILE: backend/lib/availability-search.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 46-76
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/availability-search.js#L46-L76

CODE
   46 | // Orchestrates ranges only; the existing engine still decides every bookable
   47 | // slot. Busy periods are carried within this request so buffers at a batch
   48 | // boundary do not require rereading a previously checked calendar interval.
   49 | async function searchAvailableSlots({ settings, now = DateTime.now(), policy = defaults, query = {}, readBusy, excludeSlot }) {
   50 |   policy = validateSearchPolicy(policy);
   51 |   const range = bounds({ settings, now, policy, query });
   52 |   if (range.start >= range.end) return [];
   53 |   let start = range.start;
   54 |   let end = range.specific ? range.end : DateTime.min(start.startOf("day").plus({ days: policy.initialSearchDays }), range.end);
   55 |   const periods = [], slots = [];
   56 |   let first = true;
   57 |   while (start < range.end) {
   58 |     const result = await readBusy({ start: first ? start.minus({ minutes: settings.bufferMinutes }) : start, end });
   59 |     if (!Array.isArray(result)) throw new Error("Calendar busy periods are required");
   60 |     periods.push(...result);
   61 |     const batch = findAvailableSlots({ settings, busyPeriods: periods, now: range.current,
   62 |       lookaheadDays: policy.maxBookingHorizonDays, rangeStart: start, rangeEnd: end });
   63 |     slots.push(...batch.filter(slot => !excludeSlot ||
   64 |       DateTime.fromISO(slot.start).toMillis() !== DateTime.fromISO(excludeSlot.start).toMillis() ||
   65 |       DateTime.fromISO(slot.end).toMillis() !== DateTime.fromISO(excludeSlot.end).toMillis()));
   66 |     const days = [...new Set(slots.map(slot => dayOf(slot.start, settings.timeZone)))];
   67 |     if (range.specific || days.length >= policy.targetDates) {
   68 |       const selected = new Set(days.slice(0, policy.targetDates));
   69 |       return slots.filter(slot => selected.has(dayOf(slot.start, settings.timeZone)));
   70 |     }
   71 |     start = end;
   72 |     end = DateTime.min(end.plus({ days: policy.extensionDays }), range.end);
   73 |     first = false;
   74 |   }
   75 |   return slots;
   76 | }

EXPLANATION
Reads busy periods for the first window and further windows only when needed. Carries
busy data within the request to honor buffers across batch boundaries. Stops after the
target available dates, the chosen specific day, or the maximum horizon.

   16 | function tokenPath({ booking = false } = {}) {
   17 |   const filename = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", booking
   18 |     ? process.env.GOOGLE_BOOKING_TOKEN_PATH || ".local/google-booking-token.json"
   19 |     : process.env.GOOGLE_OAUTH_TOKEN_PATH || ".local/google-token.json");

   23 |   }

   25 | }
   26 |
   27 | function loadGoogleAuthorization(options = {}) {
   28 |   const filename = tokenPath(options);
   29 |   if (!fs.existsSync(filename)) throw new Error(`Google authorization is missing. Run npm run ${options.booking ? "google:connect:booking" : "google:connect"} on your PC`);
   30 |   const saved = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filename, "utf8"));
   31 |   if (saved.type !== "authorized_user" || !saved.client_id || !saved.client_secret || !saved.refresh_token) {
   32 |     throw new Error(`Google token file is incomplete. Reauthorize locally with npm run ${options.booking ? "google:connect:booking" : "google:connect"} -- --reauthorize`);
   33 |   }
   34 |   return google.auth.fromJSON(saved);
   35 | }
   36 |
   37 | function loadCalendarClient(options = {}) {
   38 |   return google.calendar({ version: "v3", auth: loadGoogleAuthorization(options) });
   39 | }
   40 |

EXPLANATION
Resolves read-only and booking token files and refuses using the same file for both.

--- F09-B02: Normalize date text without changing its numeric meaning ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-date.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 14-19
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-date.js#L14-L19

CODE
   14 | function normalizeDateText(value) {
   15 |   // Keep numeric separators and signs: "en -3 días" must not become future +3.
   16 |   return String(value ?? "").normalize("NFD").replace(/\p{M}/gu, "").toLowerCase()
   17 |     .replace(/[¿?¡!*_~`,;()[\]{}]|\p{Extended_Pictographic}|\uFE0F/gu, " ")
   18 |     .replace(/\.(?=\s|$)/g, " ").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   19 | }

EXPLANATION
Removes comparison accents, decorative characters and excess whitespace, while keeping
numeric separators and signs. For example, a negative offset must not become a positive
one.

--- F09-B03: Validate parser context and resolved calendar dates ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-date.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 20-44
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-date.js#L20-L44

CODE
   20 | function dateError(reason, details = {}) { return { success: false, date: null, error: reason, ...details }; }
   21 | function parseAppointmentDate(value, { now = DateTime.now(), timeZone,
   22 |   maxBookingHorizonDays = readSearchPolicy().maxBookingHorizonDays } = {}) {
   23 |   const current = (typeof now === "string" ? DateTime.fromISO(now, { setZone: true }) : now).setZone(timeZone);
   24 |   if (!timeZone || !current.isValid || !Number.isInteger(maxBookingHorizonDays) || maxBookingHorizonDays < 1 || maxBookingHorizonDays > MAX_BOOKING_HORIZON_DAYS) {
   25 |     throw new Error("A valid business timezone, current time and booking horizon are required");
   26 |   }
   27 |   const today = current.startOf("day"), horizon = current.plus({ days: maxBookingHorizonDays });
   28 |   function checked(date) {
   29 |     if (!date?.isValid) return dateError("INVALID");
   30 |     if (date < today) return dateError("PAST");
   31 |     if (date >= horizon) return dateError("OUT_OF_HORIZON");
   32 |     return { success: true, date: date.toISODate(), error: null };
   33 |   }
   34 |   function calendarDate(day, month, year) {
   35 |     if (!Number.isInteger(day) || !Number.isInteger(month) || day < 1 || month < 1 || month > 12 ||
   36 |         !DateTime.fromObject({ year: 2000, month, day }, { zone: timeZone }).isValid) return dateError("INVALID");
   37 |     if (year !== undefined) return year < 1 ? dateError("INVALID") : checked(DateTime.fromObject({ year, month, day }, { zone: timeZone }));
   38 |     // Leap-day input finds its next real occurrence rather than overflowing.
   39 |     for (let candidateYear = today.year; candidateYear <= today.year + 8; candidateYear++) {
   40 |       const date = DateTime.fromObject({ year: candidateYear, month, day }, { zone: timeZone });
   41 |       if (date.isValid && date >= today) return checked(date);
   42 |     }
   43 |     return dateError("INVALID");
   44 |   }

EXPLANATION
Requires a valid business timezone and horizon. Rejects past/out-of-range results.
Explicit years are respected; a missing year searches for the next real calendar
occurrence, including leap-day handling.

--- F09-B04: Conversational prefixes, relative days and weekday rules ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-date.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 45-74
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-date.js#L45-L74

CODE
   45 |   let text = normalizeDateText(value).replace(/^(?:por favor )/, "")
   46 |     .replace(/ (?:por favor|gracias)$/, "")
   47 |     .replace(/^(?:quiero|quisiera|necesito|me gustaria|puede ser|podria ser|para)(?: para)? /, "")
   48 |     .replace(/^para /, "").replace(/^el /, "").replace(/^dia /, "");
   49 |   if (!text) return dateError("INVALID");
   50 |   if (/\b(?:o|u|y)\b/.test(text) || /^(?:algun dia|la otra semana|la semana que viene)$/.test(text)) return dateError("AMBIGUOUS");
   51 |   if (text === "hoy") return checked(today);
   52 |   if (text === "manana") return checked(today.plus({ days: 1 }));
   53 |   if (text === "pasado manana") return checked(today.plus({ days: 2 }));
   54 |   if (["ayer", "anteayer"].includes(text)) return dateError("PAST");
   55 |   let match = /^(?:en|dentro de) (\d+|[a-z]+) (dias?|semanas?)$/.exec(text);
   56 |   if (match) {
   57 |     const amount = /^\d+$/.test(match[1]) ? Number(match[1]) : numbers[match[1]];
   58 |     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(amount) || amount < 0) return dateError("INVALID");
   59 |     const days = amount * (match[2].startsWith("semana") ? 7 : 1);
   60 |     if (days > maxBookingHorizonDays) return dateError("OUT_OF_HORIZON");
   61 |     return checked(today.plus({ days }));
   62 |   }
   63 |   match = /^(?:(proximo|este) )?(lunes|martes|miercoles|jueves|viernes|sabado|domingo)( que viene| pasado)?$/.exec(text);
   64 |   if (match) {
   65 |     if (match[3] === " pasado") return dateError("PAST");
   66 |     const weekday = weekdays[match[2]], future = match[1] === "proximo" || match[3] === " que viene";
   67 |     let offset = (weekday - today.weekday + 7) % 7;
   68 |     if (future && offset === 0) offset = 7;
   69 |     const result = checked(today.plus({ days: offset }));
   70 |     // The caller checks real availability. Only a weekday meaning "today"
   71 |     // can move to next week if today's remaining slots are all unavailable.
   72 |     if (result.success && offset === 0) result.fallback = checked(today.plus({ days: 7 }));
   73 |     return result;
   74 |   }

EXPLANATION
Accepts supported polite prefixes and expressions such as mañana or en dos semanas.
Ambiguous combinations get clarification. Próximo is strictly future; a bare weekday
meaning today carries a next-week fallback for the menu to use only after checking real
availability.

--- F09-B05: Numerical, named-month and day-only dates ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-date.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 75-88
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-date.js#L75-L88

CODE
   75 |   match = /^(\d{1,2})([\/.-])(\d{1,2})(?:\2(\d{4}))?$/.exec(text) || /^(\d{1,2})( )(\d{1,2})(?: (\d{4}))?$/.exec(text);
   76 |   if (match) return calendarDate(Number(match[1]), Number(match[3]), match[4] === undefined ? undefined : Number(match[4]));
   77 |   match = /^(\d{1,2}) (?:de )?([a-z]+)(?: (?:de )?(\d{4}))?$/.exec(text);
   78 |   if (match && months[match[2]]) return calendarDate(Number(match[1]), months[match[2]], match[3] === undefined ? undefined : Number(match[3]));
   79 |   if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(text) && Number(text) >= 1 && Number(text) <= 31) {
   80 |     const day = Number(text);
   81 |     for (let offset = 0; offset <= 12; offset++) {
   82 |       const month = today.startOf("month").plus({ months: offset });
   83 |       const candidate = DateTime.fromObject({ year: month.year, month: month.month, day }, { zone: timeZone });
   84 |       if (candidate.isValid && candidate >= today) return checked(candidate);
   85 |     }
   86 |   }
   87 |   return dateError("INVALID");
   88 | }

EXPLANATION
Parses day/month order and Spanish month wording. A day without a month means its next
valid occurrence, skipping months that lack that day. Unrecognized text returns an
error; it never creates an event.

--- F10-B02: Supported phrase rules ---
FILE: backend/lib/intent-recognition.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 8-38
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/intent-recognition.js#L8-L38

CODE
    8 | const appointment = "(?:cita|turno|hora|horario|reserva|reservacion)";
    9 | const determiner = "(?:(?:una|un|la|el|mi|mis|su|tu) )?(?:(?:nueva|nuevo|otra|otro) )?";
   10 | // Small independent phrase rules; generic nouns never override explicit verbs.
   11 | const phrases = {
   12 |   BOOK: [
   13 |     "(?:agendar|agendame|reservar|reservame)",
   14 |     `(?:hacer|sacar|pedir|programar|programame|apartar|apartame) ${determiner}${appointment}`,
   15 |     `(?:quiero|quisiera|necesito|deseo|busco) ${determiner}${appointment}`,
   16 |     { phrase: `(?:nueva|otra|nuevo|otro) ${appointment}`, generic: true },
   17 |     { phrase: `${appointment} (?:nueva|nuevo)`, generic: true },
   18 |     { phrase: "disponibilidad", generic: true },
   19 |     `(?:hay|tienen|tienes) (?:(?:un|algun|alguna) )?(?:espacio|lugar|disponibilidad|horarios?)`,
   20 |   ],
   21 |   RESCHEDULE: [
   22 |     "(?:reagendar|reagendame|reprogramar|reprogramame)",
   23 |     `(?:cambiar|cambiame|mover|mueveme|posponer|adelantar|modificar|pasar) ${determiner}(?:${appointment}|fecha|dia)`,
   24 |   ],
   25 |   CANCEL: [
   26 |     "(?:cancelar|cancelame|cancela)",
   27 |     `(?:anular|anulame|eliminar|eliminame|quitar|quitame) ${determiner}${appointment}`,
   28 |     `dar de baja ${determiner}${appointment}`,
   29 |   ],
   30 | };
   31 | const rules = Object.entries(phrases).flatMap(([intent, list]) => list.map(rule => ({ intent,
   32 |   generic: rule.generic || false, pattern: new RegExp(`(?:^| )(${rule.phrase || rule})(?= |$)`, "g") })));
   33 | const unavailable = [
   34 |   /\b(?:ya )?no (?:puedo|podre|voy a poder|voy a) (?:ir|asistir|venir)\b/,
   35 |   /\bno (?:asistire|ire)\b/,
   36 |   /\bno puedo (?:manana|hoy|el (?:lunes|martes|miercoles|jueves|viernes|sabado|domingo))\b/,
   37 | ];
   38 | const alternative = /\b(?:mejor|prefiero|preferiria) (?:el )?(?:lunes|martes|miercoles|jueves|viernes|sabado|domingo|manana|otro dia|otra fecha|otro horario)\b/;

EXPLANATION
Defines booking/change/cancellation verbs and appointment nouns, plus attendance and
alternative-day patterns. These are local rules, not calls to an AI service or a general
understanding of arbitrary Spanish.

--- F10-B03: Negation handling ---
FILE: backend/lib/intent-recognition.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 39-45
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/intent-recognition.js#L39-L45

CODE
   39 | function negated(text, index) {
   40 |   // Explicit contrast starts a new clause: "no cancelar, sino reagendar".
   41 |   let prefix = text.slice(0, index).split(/\b(?:pero|sino|aunque)\b/).at(-1).trim();
   42 |   const desire = [...prefix.matchAll(/\b(?:quiero|quisiera|necesito|deseo|busco)\b/g)].at(-1);
   43 |   if (desire && !/\b(?:no|nunca|jamas)(?: me)?$/.test(prefix.slice(0, desire.index).trim())) prefix = prefix.slice(desire.index);
   44 |   return /\b(?:no|nunca|jamas|sin)(?: [\p{L}\p{N}]+){0,6}$/u.test(prefix);
   45 | }

EXPLANATION
Looks at words before a matched action to avoid treating a negated cancellation as a
request to cancel. Explicit contrast words can start a new clause.

--- F10-B04: Return one intent, clarification or unknown ---
FILE: backend/lib/intent-recognition.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 46-78
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/intent-recognition.js#L46-L78

CODE
   46 | function recognizeIntent(message, { hasUpcomingAppointment } = {}) {
   47 |   const text = normalizeMessage(message), matches = [];
   48 |   const result = (intent, matchedPhrase = null, extra = {}) => ({ intent, matchedPhrase, ...extra });
   49 |   if (!text || /\b(?:informacion|informarme|informar|politica|politicas|explicame|explicar|significa|precio|costo)\b/.test(text)) return result("UNKNOWN");
   50 |   for (const { intent, pattern, generic } of rules) {
   51 |     for (const match of text.matchAll(pattern)) {
   52 |       const index = match.index + (match[0].startsWith(" ") ? 1 : 0);
   53 |       if (!negated(text, index)) matches.push({ intent, phrase: match[1], index, generic });
   54 |     }
   55 |   }
   56 |   // "hacer otra" is reliable only alongside an explicit appointment action.
   57 |   const another = /\b(?:hacer|sacar|pedir) (?:otra|una nueva)\b/.exec(text);
   58 |   if (another && matches.some(match => match.intent !== "BOOK") && !negated(text, another.index)) {
   59 |     matches.push({ intent: "BOOK", phrase: another[0], index: another.index });
   60 |   }
   61 |   if (/^(?:cita|horarios)$/.test(text)) matches.push({ intent: "BOOK", phrase: text, index: 0 });
   62 |   const explicitChange = matches.some(match => match.intent !== "BOOK");
   63 |   const selected = matches.filter(match => !explicitChange || !match.generic);
   64 |   const intents = [...new Set(selected.map(match => match.intent))];
   65 |   if (intents.length > 1) return result("AMBIGUOUS", null, { options: intents });
   66 |   if (intents.length === 1) {
   67 |     const first = selected.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index)[0];
   68 |     return result(first.intent, first.phrase);
   69 |   }
   70 |   const attendance = unavailable.map(pattern => text.match(pattern)).find(Boolean);
   71 |   if (attendance) {
   72 |     if (hasUpcomingAppointment === undefined) return result("UNKNOWN", attendance[0], { needsAppointmentContext: true });
   73 |     if (hasUpcomingAppointment) return alternative.test(text)
   74 |       ? result("RESCHEDULE", attendance[0])
   75 |       : result("AMBIGUOUS", attendance[0], { options: ["RESCHEDULE", "CANCEL"], attendance: true });
   76 |   }
   77 |   return result("UNKNOWN");
   78 | }

EXPLANATION
Filters informational requests and negated matches, detects conflicting actions, and can
ask the caller for owned-appointment context when the customer cannot attend. The result
selects a workflow; it does not mutate a Calendar event or choose a date.

--- F11-B03: Validate the selected clarification button ---

   17 |   async function select(from, command) {
   18 |     const session = sessions.get(from);
   19 |     if (session?.mode !== "intent_clarification" || Date.now() > session.expiresAt || !session.actions?.includes(command)) {
   20 |       await sendMessage(from, "Esta opción ya no está vigente. " + MENU_INSTRUCTION);
   21 |       return null;
   22 |     }
   23 |     return command.split(":").at(-1).toUpperCase();
   24 |   }
   25 |   return { offer, select };
   26 | }

EXPLANATION
Only a current unexpired button that was actually issued can return an action. A
guessed, old or unrelated ID receives stale-option guidance.

--- F12-B03: Split long appointment summaries ---
FILE: backend/lib/appointment-messages.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 24-36
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/appointment-messages.js#L24-L36

CODE
   24 | // Long appointment overviews are sent as text before the menu buttons, so all
   25 | // upcoming appointments fit without exceeding WhatsApp's interactive body limit.
   26 | function textChunks(body, limit = 3500) {
   27 |   const chunks = [];
   28 |   while (body.length > limit) {
   29 |     const lastLine = body.lastIndexOf("\n", limit);
   30 |     const cut = lastLine > 0 ? lastLine : limit;
   31 |     chunks.push(body.slice(0, cut));
   32 |     body = body.slice(cut).replace(/^\n/, "");
   33 |   }
   34 |   if (body) chunks.push(body);
   35 |   return chunks;
   36 | }

EXPLANATION
Splits text into WhatsApp-sized chunks, preferring newline boundaries. This keeps every
appointment visible when the overview cannot fit inside the button message.

Single date/time-label format for all active appointment choices.

--- F13-B01: Business-local compact Spanish label ---
FILE: backend/lib/slot-label.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1-13
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/slot-label.js#L1-L13

CODE
    1 | "use strict";
    2 |
    3 | const { DateTime } = require("luxon");
    4 |
    5 | function slotLabel(iso, timeZone) {
    6 |   const date = DateTime.fromISO(iso, { zone: timeZone }).setZone(timeZone).setLocale("es");
    7 |   if (!date.isValid) throw new Error("A valid slot timestamp and business timezone are required");
    8 |   const label = date.toFormat("ccc d LLL HH:mm").toLowerCase();
    9 |   if (label.length > 20) throw new Error("WhatsApp slot label exceeds 20 characters");
   10 |   return label;
   11 | }
   12 |
   13 | module.exports = { slotLabel };

EXPLANATION
Converts the full timestamp using the supplied business timezone, then produces
lowercase ccc d LLL HH:mm: for example, mié 23 jun 11:00. An invalid date/timezone or
output beyond 20 characters is rejected. This changes only the label, never the stored
slot or action ID.

    3 | // Use only where returning to the main menu helps the customer recover or exit.
    4 | const MENU_INSTRUCTION = 'Escribe "menu" para volver al menú principal.';
    5 |
    6 | module.exports = { MENU_INSTRUCTION };

EXPLANATION
Defines Escribe "menu" para volver al menú principal. Callers use it only where useful;
it is not automatically added to every outgoing message.

--- F15-B01: Check the signature against the exact received bytes ---
FILE: backend/lib/meta-signature.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1-12
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/meta-signature.js#L1-L12

CODE
    1 | "use strict";
    2 |
    3 | const { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } = require("node:crypto");
    4 |
    5 | function validMetaSignature(rawBody, signature, secret) {
    6 |   if (!Buffer.isBuffer(rawBody) || !secret || typeof signature !== "string" || !/^sha256=[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(signature)) return false;
    7 |   const actual = Buffer.from(signature.slice(7), "hex");
    8 |   const expected = createHmac("sha256", secret).update(rawBody).digest();
    9 |   return actual.length === expected.length && timingSafeEqual(actual, expected);
   10 | }
   11 |
   12 | module.exports = { validMetaSignature };

EXPLANATION
Requires the expected SHA-256 signature shape, computes the HMAC using the local app
secret and compares bytes in constant time. The main POST handler rejects the request
when this returns false. This verifies the request source, not the customer's legal
identity.

--- F16-B01: Imports and all-or-nothing local transactions ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1-9
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L1-L9

CODE
    1 | "use strict";
    2 | const { createHash, randomUUID } = require("node:crypto");
    3 | const os = require("node:os");
    4 |
    5 | function transaction(db, action) {
    6 |   db.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
    7 |   try { const value = action(); db.exec("COMMIT"); return value; }
    8 |   catch (error) { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); throw error; }
    9 | }

EXPLANATION
A local transaction commits related database changes together or rolls them back on
failure. This helper does not turn a separate Google/Meta network request into an atomic
database operation.

--- F16-B02: SETUP ONLY: durable message and worker tables ---

   10 | function prepareReliabilitySchema(db) {
   11 |   transaction(db, () => db.exec(`
   12 |     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS message_inbox (
   13 |       inbox_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   14 |       business_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES businesses(business_id),
   15 |       message_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   16 |       sender TEXT NOT NULL,
   17 |       phone_number_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   18 |       message_json TEXT NOT NULL,
   19 |       content_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
   20 |       capabilities TEXT NOT NULL,
   21 |       state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('queued','processing','completed','blocked')),
   22 |       received_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   23 |       customer_time INTEGER NOT NULL,
   24 |       attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
   25 |       next_attempt_at INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
   26 |       snapshot_json TEXT,
   27 |       continuation_json TEXT,
   28 |       last_error TEXT,
   29 |       UNIQUE(business_id,message_id)
   30 |     );
   31 |     CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inbox_customer ON message_inbox(business_id,sender,inbox_id,state);
   32 |     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS message_outbox (
   33 |       outbox_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
   34 |       inbox_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES message_inbox(inbox_id),
   35 |       ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL,
   36 |       payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
   37 |       state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('pending','sending','accepted','uncertain','failed','expired')),
   38 |       attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
   39 |       next_attempt_at INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
   40 |       expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   41 |       meta_message_id TEXT,
   42 |       meta_status TEXT,
   43 |       last_error TEXT,
   44 |       UNIQUE(inbox_id,ordinal)
   45 |     );
   46 |     CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbox_pending ON message_outbox(state,next_attempt_at,inbox_id,ordinal);
   47 |     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS delivery_worker (
   48 |       singleton INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton=1),
   49 |       host TEXT NOT NULL,
   50 |       pid INTEGER NOT NULL,
   51 |       owner TEXT NOT NULL
   52 |     );
   53 |   `));
   54 | }

EXPLANATION
The explicit preparation command creates the inbox, outbox and one-worker ownership
