--- F16-B03: Normalize the recipient and accept supported message shapes ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 55-65
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L55-L65

CODE
   55 | function normalizedRecipient(sender) { return /^521\d{10}$/.test(sender) ? "52" + sender.slice(3) : sender; }
   56 | function supportedMessage(message) {
   57 |   if (!message || typeof message.from !== "string" || !message.from || typeof message.id !== "string" || !message.id) return null;
   58 |   const base = { id: message.id, from: message.from, type: message.type };
   59 |   if (message.type === "text" && typeof message.text?.body === "string") return { ...base, text: { body: message.text.body } };
   60 |   const kind = message.interactive?.type;
   61 |   if (message.type === "interactive" && ["button_reply", "list_reply"].includes(kind) && typeof message.interactive[kind]?.id === "string") {
   62 |     return { ...base, interactive: { type: kind, [kind]: { id: message.interactive[kind].id } } };
   63 |   }
   64 |   return null;
   65 | }

EXPLANATION
Keeps stable sender/message IDs and supported text/button/list content. Other message
types are ignored; malformed messages of supported types cannot be treated as safely
recorded.

--- F16-B04: Ensure exactly one local worker owns this database ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 66-88
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L66-L88

CODE
   66 | function createReliableDelivery({ db, capabilities, resolveBusiness, now = Date.now,
   67 |   processAlive = pid => { try { process.kill(pid, 0); return true; } catch (error) { return error.code !== "ESRCH"; } },
   68 |   host = os.hostname(), pid = process.pid }) {
   69 |   let owner;
   70 |   function assertOwner() {
   71 |     const current = db.prepare("SELECT owner FROM delivery_worker WHERE singleton=1").get();
   72 |     if (!owner || current?.owner !== owner) throw new Error("Reliable delivery worker ownership was lost");
   73 |   }
   74 |   function acquire() {
   75 |     transaction(db, () => {
   76 |       const previous = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM delivery_worker WHERE singleton=1").get();
   77 |       if (previous && (previous.host !== host || processAlive(previous.pid))) throw new Error("Another reliability worker owns this database; stop that process first");
   78 |       owner = randomUUID();
   79 |       db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO delivery_worker VALUES (1,?,?,?)").run(host, pid, owner);
   80 |       // No time-based lease stealing: an in-flight Calendar write may still be
   81 |       // running. Recovery is allowed only after the local owner process is dead.
   82 |       db.exec("UPDATE message_inbox SET state='queued' WHERE state='processing'; UPDATE message_outbox SET state='uncertain',last_error='restart_during_send' WHERE state='sending';");
   83 |     });
   84 |   }
   85 |   function release() {
   86 |     if (owner) db.prepare("DELETE FROM delivery_worker WHERE owner=?").run(owner);
   87 |     owner = undefined;
   88 |   }

EXPLANATION
Stores host/process ownership and refuses another live owner. Restart recovery can claim
a dead local worker's records, requeue interrupted inputs and mark interrupted sends
uncertain. It does not steal a time-based lease from a process that might still be
writing to Google.

--- F16-B05: Use signed Meta delivery-status callbacks ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 89-107
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L89-L107

CODE
   89 |   function receipt(business, status) {
   90 |     const reference = status?.biz_opaque_callback_data;
   91 |     if (typeof reference !== "string" || !reference.startsWith("nukmiris:")) return;
   92 |     const row = db.prepare(`SELECT o.*,i.sender,i.business_id,i.phone_number_id FROM message_outbox o
   93 |       JOIN message_inbox i USING(inbox_id) WHERE o.outbox_id=?`).get(reference.slice(9));
   94 |     if (!row || row.business_id !== business.business_id || row.phone_number_id !== business.phone_number_id ||
   95 |         typeof status.id !== "string" || (row.meta_message_id && row.meta_message_id !== status.id) ||
   96 |         normalizedRecipient(status.recipient_id || "") !== normalizedRecipient(row.sender)) return;
   97 |     if (["sent", "delivered", "read"].includes(status.status)) {
   98 |       const rank = { sent: 1, failed: 2, delivered: 2, read: 3 };
   99 |       if ((rank[row.meta_status] || 0) <= rank[status.status]) {
  100 |         db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state='accepted',meta_message_id=?,meta_status=?,last_error=NULL WHERE outbox_id=?")
  101 |           .run(status.id, status.status, row.outbox_id);
  102 |       }
  103 |     } else if (status.status === "failed" && !["delivered", "read"].includes(row.meta_status)) {
  104 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state='failed',meta_message_id=?,meta_status='failed',last_error='meta_delivery_failed' WHERE outbox_id=?")
  105 |         .run(status.id, row.outbox_id);
  106 |     }
  107 |   }

EXPLANATION
Matches the callback to its saved output, business, receiving number and customer.
Sent/delivered/read statuses can prove acceptance after a lost send response. Unknown or
mismatched references are ignored.

--- F16-B06: Save and deduplicate incoming messages before acknowledgement ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 108-146
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L108-L146

CODE
  108 |   function accept(body) {
  109 |     assertOwner();
  110 |     if (!Array.isArray(body?.entry)) throw Object.assign(new Error("Invalid webhook shape"), { status: 400 });
  111 |     return transaction(db, () => {
  112 |       let inserted = 0;
  113 |       for (const entry of body.entry) {
  114 |         if (!Array.isArray(entry.changes)) continue;
  115 |         for (const change of entry.changes) {
  116 |           const phone = change.value?.metadata?.phone_number_id;
  117 |           const business = typeof phone === "string" ? resolveBusiness(phone) : null;
  118 |           if (!business) continue;
  119 |           for (const status of Array.isArray(change.value?.statuses) ? change.value.statuses : []) receipt(business, status);
  120 |           for (const raw of Array.isArray(change.value?.messages) ? change.value.messages : []) {
  121 |             const message = supportedMessage(raw);
  122 |             if (!message) {
  123 |               if (raw?.type === "text" || (raw?.type === "interactive" && ["button_reply", "list_reply"].includes(raw.interactive?.type))) {
  124 |                 throw Object.assign(new Error("A supported message needs a stable ID and sender"), { status: 400 });
  125 |               }
  126 |               continue;
  127 |             }
  128 |             const json = JSON.stringify(message), hash = createHash("sha256").update(json).digest("hex");
  129 |             const existing = db.prepare("SELECT content_hash FROM message_inbox WHERE business_id=? AND message_id=?").get(business.business_id, message.id);
  130 |             if (existing) {
  131 |               if (existing.content_hash !== hash) throw Object.assign(new Error("Conflicting message identity"), { status: 409 });
  132 |               continue;
  133 |             }
  134 |             const timestamp = Number(raw.timestamp) * 1000;
  135 |             const customerTime = Number.isFinite(timestamp) && timestamp > 0 && timestamp <= now() + 300000 ? timestamp : now();
  136 |             db.prepare(`INSERT INTO message_inbox
  137 |               (business_id,message_id,sender,phone_number_id,message_json,content_hash,capabilities,state,received_at,customer_time)
  138 |               VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'queued',?,?)`).run(business.business_id, message.id, message.from,
  139 |                 business.phone_number_id, json, hash, capabilities, now(), customerTime);
  140 |             inserted++;
  141 |           }
  142 |         }
  143 |       }
  144 |       return inserted;
  145 |     });
  146 |   }

EXPLANATION
Within one transaction, resolves the receiving business and stores valid inputs. A
repeated ID with identical content is ignored; the same ID with different content is
rejected. Stores the receive/customer time used for reply expiry.

--- F16-B07: Revalidate the business and restore interrupted local state ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 147-167
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L147-L167

CODE
  147 |   function businessForRow(row) {
  148 |     const business = resolveBusiness(row.phone_number_id);
  149 |     if (!business || business.business_id !== row.business_id || row.capabilities !== capabilities) return null;
  150 |     return business;
  151 |   }
  152 |   function restoreSnapshot(row) {
  153 |     const saved = JSON.parse(row.snapshot_json);
  154 |     db.prepare("DELETE FROM business_customer_sessions WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?").run(row.business_id, row.sender);
  155 |     if (saved.session) db.prepare("INSERT INTO business_customer_sessions VALUES (?,?,?,?)")
  156 |       .run(row.business_id, row.sender, saved.session.session_json, saved.session.expires_at);
  157 |     if (saved.support) {
  158 |       const s = saved.support;
  159 |       db.prepare(`INSERT INTO business_support_conversations VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
  160 |         ON CONFLICT(business_id,whatsapp_id) DO UPDATE SET customer_id=excluded.customer_id,status=excluded.status,
  161 |         assigned_to=excluded.assigned_to,created_at=excluded.created_at,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`)
  162 |         .run(s.business_id, s.whatsapp_id, s.customer_id, s.status, s.assigned_to, s.created_at, s.updated_at);
  163 |     } else {
  164 |       db.prepare("DELETE FROM business_support_messages WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=? AND id=?").run(row.business_id, row.sender, row.message_id);
  165 |       db.prepare("DELETE FROM business_support_conversations WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?").run(row.business_id, row.sender);
  166 |     }
  167 |   }

EXPLANATION
Saved work must still match its original business and enabled capabilities. If a handler
crashed before an operation checkpoint or output commit, its saved conversation/support
snapshot is restored before replay.

--- F16-B08: Claim the next eligible input in customer order ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 168-192
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L168-L192

CODE
  168 |   function claimInput() {
  169 |     assertOwner();
  170 |     return transaction(db, () => {
  171 |       const row = db.prepare(`SELECT i.* FROM message_inbox i WHERE i.state='queued' AND i.next_attempt_at<=?
  172 |         AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM message_inbox old WHERE old.business_id=i.business_id AND old.sender=i.sender
  173 |           AND old.inbox_id<i.inbox_id AND (old.state<>'completed' OR EXISTS
  174 |             (SELECT 1 FROM message_outbox o WHERE o.inbox_id=old.inbox_id AND o.state NOT IN ('accepted','expired'))))
  175 |         ORDER BY i.inbox_id LIMIT 1`).get(now());
  176 |       if (!row) return null;
  177 |       const business = businessForRow(row);
  178 |       if (!business) {
  179 |         db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET state='blocked',last_error='configuration_changed' WHERE inbox_id=?").run(row.inbox_id);
  180 |         return { blocked: true };
  181 |       }
  182 |       if (row.snapshot_json && !row.continuation_json) restoreSnapshot(row);
  183 |       if (!row.snapshot_json) {
  184 |         row.snapshot_json = JSON.stringify({
  185 |           session: db.prepare("SELECT * FROM business_customer_sessions WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?").get(row.business_id, row.sender) || null,
  186 |           support: db.prepare("SELECT * FROM business_support_conversations WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?").get(row.business_id, row.sender) || null,
  187 |         });
  188 |       }
  189 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET state='processing',snapshot_json=?,attempts=attempts+1 WHERE inbox_id=?").run(row.snapshot_json, row.inbox_id);
  190 |       return { ...row, business, message: JSON.parse(row.message_json), continuation: row.continuation_json ? JSON.parse(row.continuation_json) : null };
  191 |     });
  192 |   }

EXPLANATION
Does not allow a later message for the same business/customer to overtake an earlier
unfinished input or unresolved reply. Saves a snapshot and increments attempts before
handing the message to the router.

--- F16-B09: Save an operation continuation ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 193-202
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L193-L202

CODE
  193 |   function checkpoint(inboxId, continuation, saveState = () => {}) {
  194 |     assertOwner();
  195 |     transaction(db, () => {
  196 |       const row = db.prepare("SELECT state,continuation_json FROM message_inbox WHERE inbox_id=?").get(inboxId);
  197 |       const json = JSON.stringify(continuation);
  198 |       if (row?.state !== "processing" || (row.continuation_json && row.continuation_json !== json)) throw new Error("Invalid message continuation");
  199 |       saveState();
  200 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET continuation_json=? WHERE inbox_id=?").run(json, inboxId);
  201 |     });
  202 |   }

EXPLANATION
Atomically binds the processing input to the booking/change request and optional new
session state. Recovery can then resume the saved operation instead of repeating earlier
steps that could create another request.

--- F16-B10: Save all replies and complete their input together ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 203-222
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L203-L222

CODE
  203 |   function outputs(inboxId, payloads) {
  204 |     assertOwner();
  205 |     return transaction(db, () => {
  206 |       const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM message_inbox WHERE inbox_id=? AND state='processing'").get(inboxId);
  207 |       if (!row || !payloads.length) throw new Error("Output must belong to the current message");
  208 |       const last = db.prepare("SELECT MAX(customer_time) AS t FROM message_inbox WHERE business_id=? AND sender=?").get(row.business_id, row.sender).t;
  209 |       const session = db.prepare("SELECT expires_at FROM business_customer_sessions WHERE business_id=? AND whatsapp_id=?").get(row.business_id, row.sender);
  210 |       for (const [ordinal, payload] of payloads.entries()) {
  211 |         if (payload.to !== normalizedRecipient(row.sender)) throw new Error("Reply recipient does not match the incoming customer");
  212 |         const id = randomUUID();
  213 |         const buttons = payload.interactive?.action.buttons?.map(item => item.reply.id) || [];
  214 |         const rows = payload.interactive?.action.sections?.flatMap(section => section.rows.map(item => item.id)) || [];
  215 |         const browsing = [...buttons, ...rows].some(value => /^(availability|menu|manage|owned|intent):/.test(value));
  216 |         const expires = Math.min(last + 24 * 3600000, browsing && session ? session.expires_at : Infinity);
  217 |         db.prepare("INSERT INTO message_outbox (outbox_id,inbox_id,ordinal,payload_json,state,expires_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'pending',?)")
  218 |           .run(id, inboxId, ordinal, JSON.stringify({ ...payload, biz_opaque_callback_data: `nukmiris:${id}` }), expires);
  219 |       }
  220 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET state='completed',last_error=NULL WHERE inbox_id=?").run(inboxId);
  221 |     });
  222 |   }

EXPLANATION
Verifies the recipient, stores ordered payloads with correlation IDs and sets expiry
from the service window and current browsing offer. Output records and input completion
are committed together; actual network delivery happens separately.

--- F16-B11: Complete no-reply inputs and handle processing failures ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 223-232
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L223-L232

CODE
  223 |   function complete(inboxId) {
  224 |     assertOwner();
  225 |     db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET state='completed',last_error=NULL WHERE inbox_id=? AND state='processing'").run(inboxId);
  226 |   }
  227 |   function inputFailed(inboxId) {
  228 |     assertOwner();
  229 |     const row = db.prepare("SELECT attempts,state FROM message_inbox WHERE inbox_id=?").get(inboxId);
  230 |     if (row?.state === "processing") db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET state=?,next_attempt_at=?,last_error='handler_failed' WHERE inbox_id=?")
  231 |       .run(row.attempts >= 5 ? "blocked" : "queued", now() + Math.min(60000, 1000 * 2 ** row.attempts), inboxId);
  232 |   }

EXPLANATION
Marks a successfully handled input complete even when it produced no outgoing reply.
Failures retry with backoff; after the allowed attempts they remain blocked for review
rather than being silently discarded.

--- F16-B12: Claim an outgoing reply and record its result ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 233-261
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L233-L261

CODE
  233 |   function claimOutput() {
  234 |     assertOwner();
  235 |     return transaction(db, () => {
  236 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state='expired',last_error='reply_expired' WHERE state='pending' AND expires_at<=?").run(now());
  237 |       const row = db.prepare(`SELECT o.*,i.business_id,i.sender,i.phone_number_id,i.capabilities FROM message_outbox o
  238 |         JOIN message_inbox i USING(inbox_id) WHERE o.state='pending' AND o.next_attempt_at<=?
  239 |         AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM message_outbox old JOIN message_inbox oi USING(inbox_id)
  240 |           WHERE oi.business_id=i.business_id AND oi.sender=i.sender AND old.state NOT IN ('accepted','expired')
  241 |           AND (old.inbox_id<o.inbox_id OR (old.inbox_id=o.inbox_id AND old.ordinal<o.ordinal)))
  242 |         ORDER BY o.inbox_id,o.ordinal LIMIT 1`).get(now());
  243 |       if (!row) return null;
  244 |       if (!businessForRow(row)) {
  245 |         db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state='failed',last_error='configuration_changed' WHERE outbox_id=?").run(row.outbox_id);
  246 |         return { blocked: true };
  247 |       }
  248 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state='sending',attempts=attempts+1 WHERE outbox_id=?").run(row.outbox_id);
  249 |       return { ...row, payload: JSON.parse(row.payload_json) };
  250 |     });
  251 |   }
  252 |   function outputResult(row, result) {
  253 |     assertOwner();
  254 |     // A signed Meta status can arrive before the HTTP response. Never overwrite
  255 |     // that proof of acceptance with a later timeout/error from the POST call.
  256 |     if (result.state === "accepted") {
  257 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state='accepted',meta_message_id=?,last_error=NULL WHERE outbox_id=? AND state='sending'")
  258 |         .run(result.metaMessageId, row.outbox_id);
  259 |     } else db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state=?,next_attempt_at=?,last_error=? WHERE outbox_id=? AND state='sending'")
  260 |       .run(result.state, now() + Math.min(600000, 1000 * 2 ** Math.min(row.attempts + 1, 10)), result.error, row.outbox_id);
  261 |   }

EXPLANATION
Expires old pending replies and preserves reply order per customer. Rechecks original
routing/capabilities before sending. A later failed HTTP response cannot erase
acceptance already established by a signed status callback.

--- F16-B13: Explicit business-scoped operator retry ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 262-280
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L262-L280

CODE
  262 |   function retry({ businessId, uncertain = false } = {}) {
  263 |     if (!businessId) throw new Error("Select the business whose messages need retry");
  264 |     transaction(db, () => {
  265 |       db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET state='queued',attempts=0,next_attempt_at=0 WHERE business_id=? AND state='blocked' AND last_error='handler_failed'").run(businessId);
  266 |       for (const row of db.prepare("SELECT * FROM message_inbox WHERE business_id=? AND state='blocked' AND last_error='configuration_changed'").all(businessId)) {
  267 |         if (businessForRow(row)) db.prepare("UPDATE message_inbox SET state='queued',attempts=0,next_attempt_at=0,last_error=NULL WHERE inbox_id=?").run(row.inbox_id);
  268 |       }
  269 |       for (const row of db.prepare(`SELECT o.outbox_id,i.* FROM message_outbox o JOIN message_inbox i USING(inbox_id)
  270 |         WHERE i.business_id=? AND o.state='failed' AND o.last_error='configuration_changed'`).all(businessId)) {
  271 |         if (businessForRow(row)) db.prepare("UPDATE message_outbox SET state='pending',next_attempt_at=0,last_error=NULL WHERE outbox_id=?").run(row.outbox_id);
  272 |       }
  273 |       db.prepare(`UPDATE message_outbox SET state='pending',next_attempt_at=0 WHERE inbox_id IN
  274 |         (SELECT inbox_id FROM message_inbox WHERE business_id=?) AND
  275 |         ((state='failed' AND last_error<>'configuration_changed') ${uncertain ? "OR state='uncertain'" : ""})`).run(businessId);
  276 |     });
  277 |   }
  278 |   return { acquire, release, accept, claimInput, checkpoint, outputs, complete, inputFailed, claimOutput, outputResult, retry };
  279 | }
  280 |

EXPLANATION
Requeues eligible blocked/rejected work for the selected business. Uncertain sends are
included only when explicitly requested; retrying those may duplicate a WhatsApp message
already accepted by Meta. This method does not itself perform Calendar mutations.

--- F16-B14: Send one saved reply and classify the outcome ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 281-295
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L281-L295

CODE
  281 | async function sendStoredReply({ row, token, apiVersion = "v26.0", request = fetch }) {
  282 |   try {
  283 |     const response = await request(`https://graph.facebook.com/${apiVersion}/${row.phone_number_id}/messages`, {
  284 |       method: "POST", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  285 |       body: JSON.stringify(row.payload), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),
  286 |     });
  287 |     let data;
  288 |     try { data = await response.json(); } catch { return { state: "uncertain", error: "unreadable_meta_response" }; }
  289 |     if (response.ok && typeof data.messages?.[0]?.id === "string") return { state: "accepted", metaMessageId: data.messages[0].id };
  290 |     if (!response.ok && data.error && !data.messages) {
  291 |       return { state: response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500 || data.error.is_transient ? "pending" : "failed", error: `meta_http_${response.status}` };
  292 |     }
  293 |     return { state: "uncertain", error: "unverified_meta_response" };
  294 |   } catch { return { state: "uncertain", error: "meta_transport_unknown" }; }
  295 | }

EXPLANATION
Uses the original business receiving number, a timeout and the stored payload. Verified
acceptance, known API rejection and unknown delivery are different states. A timeout or
unreadable response is uncertain, not proof that the message was never delivered.

--- F16-B15: Worker loop and scheduling ---
FILE: backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 296-334
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/reliable-delivery.js#L296-L334

CODE
  296 | function createDeliveryWorker({ delivery, handle, resume, runInContext, send, intervalMs = 1000, onError = () => {} }) {
  297 |   let stopped = true, timer, active;
  298 |   async function drain() {
  299 |     if (active) return active;
  300 |     active = (async () => {
  301 |       let progressed;
  302 |       do {
  303 |         progressed = false;
  304 |         const reply = delivery.claimOutput();
  305 |         if (reply) {
  306 |           progressed = true;
  307 |           if (!reply.blocked) delivery.outputResult(reply, await send(reply));
  308 |         }
  309 |         if (stopped) break;
  310 |         const input = delivery.claimInput();
  311 |         if (input) {
  312 |           progressed = true;
  313 |           if (!input.blocked) {
  314 |             try {
  315 |               await runInContext(input, () => input.continuation ? resume(input) : handle(input));
  316 |               delivery.complete(input.inbox_id);
  317 |             } catch { delivery.inputFailed(input.inbox_id); onError(); }
  318 |           }
  319 |         }
  320 |       } while (progressed && !stopped);
  321 |     })().catch(onError).finally(() => { active = undefined; });
  322 |     return active;
  323 |   }
  324 |   function schedule() {
  325 |     if (stopped || timer) return;
  326 |     timer = setTimeout(async () => { timer = undefined; await drain(); schedule(); }, intervalMs);
  327 |     timer.unref();
  328 |   }
  329 |   function start() { stopped = false; schedule(); }
  330 |   function wake() { if (!stopped) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = undefined; void drain().then(schedule); } }
  331 |   async function stop() { stopped = true; clearTimeout(timer); timer = undefined; await active; }
  332 |   return { start, wake, stop, drain };
  333 | }
  334 | module.exports = { prepareReliabilitySchema, createReliableDelivery, createDeliveryWorker, sendStoredReply, normalizedRecipient };

EXPLANATION
Runs one drain at a time, sends eligible output, then processes the next input or
resumes its saved operation in the proper business context. start/wake/stop manage the
loop. This is a local single-worker design, not distributed hosting.

    7 | const backupName = /^nukmiris-(\d+)-[0-9a-f-]{36}\.sqlite\.bak$/;
    8 | const countedTables = ["businesses", "business_customers", "business_customer_sessions", "business_support_conversations",

--- F17-B02: Verify database integrity and relationships ---
FILE: backend/lib/database-backups.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 10-24
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/database-backups.js#L10-L24

CODE
   10 | function inspectDatabase(filename) {
   11 |   const db = new DatabaseSync(filename, { readOnly: true });
   12 |   try {
   13 |     const integrity = db.prepare("PRAGMA integrity_check").all();
   14 |     if (integrity.length !== 1 || Object.values(integrity[0])[0] !== "ok" || db.prepare("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all().length) {
   15 |       throw new Error("Database integrity verification failed");
   16 |     }
   17 |     const counts = {};
   18 |     for (const name of countedTables) {
   19 |       if (db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?").get(name)) counts[name] = db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM ${name}`).get().n;
   20 |     }
   21 |     if (!counts.businesses) throw new Error("A NukMiris business database is required");
   22 |     return counts;
   23 |   } finally { db.close(); }
   24 | }

EXPLANATION
Opens a snapshot read-only, checks SQLite integrity/foreign keys and counts application
tables. A real business table must exist; an arbitrary empty file is not a valid
application backup.

--- F17-B03: List and create verified backups ---
FILE: backend/lib/database-backups.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 25-44
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/database-backups.js#L25-L44

CODE
   25 | function listBackups(directory) {
   26 |   if (!fs.existsSync(directory)) return [];
   27 |   return fs.readdirSync(directory).filter(name => backupName.test(name)).map(name => ({
   28 |     filename: path.join(directory, name), createdAt: Number(backupName.exec(name)[1]),
   29 |   })).sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt || a.filename.localeCompare(b.filename));
   30 | }
   31 | async function createDatabaseBackup({ db, directory, keep = 7, now = Date.now }) {
   32 |   if (!Number.isInteger(keep) || keep < 2 || keep > 90) throw new Error("Keep between 2 and 90 verified backups");
   33 |   fs.mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
   34 |   const filename = path.join(directory, `nukmiris-${now()}-${randomUUID()}.sqlite.bak`), temporary = `${filename}.partial`;
   35 |   try {
   36 |     await backup(db, temporary);
   37 |     fs.chmodSync(temporary, 0o600);
   38 |     const counts = inspectDatabase(temporary);
   39 |     fs.renameSync(temporary, filename);
   40 |     // Prune only this tool's backup names, and only after a verified replacement.
   41 |     for (const old of listBackups(directory).slice(keep)) fs.unlinkSync(old.filename);
   42 |     return { filename, counts };
   43 |   } finally { if (fs.existsSync(temporary)) fs.unlinkSync(temporary); }
   44 | }

EXPLANATION
Creates a consistent SQLite snapshot as a temporary file, verifies it and then gives it
its final name. Only after a verified replacement exists are older matching backups
pruned according to retention.

--- F17-B04: Restore to a separate unused file ---
FILE: backend/lib/database-backups.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 45-60
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/database-backups.js#L45-L60

CODE
   45 | async function restoreDatabaseCopy({ source, destination }) {
   46 |   if (path.resolve(source) === path.resolve(destination) || fs.existsSync(destination)) throw new Error("Restore requires a new, unused destination; existing databases are never replaced");
   47 |   const expected = inspectDatabase(source);
   48 |   fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(destination), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
   49 |   const temporary = `${destination}.${randomUUID()}.partial`;
   50 |   const db = new DatabaseSync(source, { readOnly: true });
   51 |   try {
   52 |     await backup(db, temporary);
   53 |     const counts = inspectDatabase(temporary);
   54 |     if (JSON.stringify(counts) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) throw new Error("Restored row counts do not match the backup");
   55 |     fs.copyFileSync(temporary, destination, fs.constants.COPYFILE_EXCL);
   56 |     fs.chmodSync(destination, 0o600);
   57 |     inspectDatabase(destination);
   58 |     return { filename: destination, counts };
   59 |   } finally { db.close(); if (fs.existsSync(temporary)) fs.unlinkSync(temporary); }
   60 | }

EXPLANATION
Checks the source, refuses overwriting an existing destination, restores to a temporary
file and compares integrity/relationships/counts. The result is a new copy; this
function does not replace the running database or undo Calendar events.

--- F17-B05: Automatic backup schedule ---
FILE: backend/lib/database-backups.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 61-83
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/database-backups.js#L61-L83

CODE
   61 | function createBackupSchedule({ db, directory, intervalHours = 24, keep = 7, now = Date.now, onError = () => {} }) {
   62 |   if (!Number.isFinite(intervalHours) || intervalHours < 1 || intervalHours > 168) throw new Error("Backup interval must be 1 to 168 hours");
   63 |   if (!Number.isInteger(keep) || keep < 2 || keep > 90) throw new Error("Keep between 2 and 90 verified backups");
   64 |   let active, timer, stopped = true, lastError = false;
   65 |   async function ensure() {
   66 |     if (active) return active;
   67 |     active = (async () => {
   68 |       const last = listBackups(directory)[0];
   69 |       let verified = false;
   70 |       if (last) { try { inspectDatabase(last.filename); verified = true; } catch { /* Replace a corrupt backup without removing older copies first. */ } }
   71 |       if (!verified || now() - last.createdAt >= intervalHours * 3600000) await createDatabaseBackup({ db, directory, keep, now });
   72 |       lastError = false;
   73 |     })().catch(error => { lastError = true; throw error; }).finally(() => { active = undefined; });
   74 |     return active;
   75 |   }
   76 |   function start() {
   77 |     stopped = false;
   78 |     timer = setInterval(() => { if (!stopped) void ensure().catch(onError); }, 60000);
   79 |     timer.unref();
   80 |   }
   81 |   async function stop() { stopped = true; clearInterval(timer); await active?.catch(() => {}); }
   82 |   return { ensure, start, stop, status: () => ({ lastError, latest: listBackups(directory)[0]?.createdAt || null }) };
   83 | }

EXPLANATION
Checks whether a verified recent backup exists and creates one if needed. A minute timer
checks the configured interval while the app runs. The default 24-hour setting is a due
interval, not a separate always-on backup server.

--- F19-B01: Check the configured Meta sender identity ---
FILE: backend/lib/connection-checks.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1-13
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/connection-checks.js#L1-L13

    1 | "use strict";
    2 |
    3 | async function checkMeta({ token, phoneNumberId, apiVersion = "v26.0", request = fetch }) {
    4 |   if (!token || !phoneNumberId) throw new Error("Set the local WhatsApp token and phone-number ID first");
    5 |   const response = await request(`https://graph.facebook.com/${encodeURIComponent(apiVersion)}/${encodeURIComponent(phoneNumberId)}?fields=id`, {
    6 |     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),
    7 |   });
    8 |   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Meta access check failed (HTTP ${response.status}); review your local token and phone-number ID`);
    9 |   const result = await response.json();
   10 |   if (result.id !== phoneNumberId) throw new Error("Meta returned a different phone-number ID");
   11 |   return true;
   12 | }
   13 |

EXPLANATION
Makes a timed read request for the configured receiving phone-number ID and checks the
returned ID. This does not send a WhatsApp message or perform the webhook verification
handshake.

--- F19-B02: List accessible calendars with write access ---
FILE: backend/lib/connection-checks.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 14-26
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/connection-checks.js#L14-L26

CODE
   14 | async function listWritableCalendars(client) {
   15 |   const choices = [];
   16 |   let pageToken;
   17 |   do {
   18 |     const result = await client.calendarList.list({ maxResults: 250, ...(pageToken ? { pageToken } : {}) });
   19 |     for (const item of result.data.items || []) {
   20 |       if (item.id && ["owner", "writer"].includes(item.accessRole)) choices.push({ id: item.id, name: item.summary || item.id });
   21 |     }
   22 |     pageToken = result.data.nextPageToken;
   23 |   } while (pageToken);
   24 |   return choices;
   25 | }
   26 |

EXPLANATION
Reads all Calendar-list pages and returns calendars where the account is an owner or
writer. It does not select primary automatically or change an event.

--- F21-B01: Read Desktop OAuth setup and generate one-time checks ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking-oauth.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 1-17
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking-oauth.js#L1-L17

CODE
    1 | "use strict";
    2 |
    3 | const fs = require("node:fs");
    4 | const http = require("node:http");
    5 | const { randomBytes, createHash, timingSafeEqual } = require("node:crypto");
    6 | const { google } = require("googleapis");
    7 | const { BOOKING_SCOPES } = require("./google-calendar");
    8 |
    9 | async function authenticateBooking({ credentialsPath, timeoutMs = 5 * 60 * 1000,
   10 |   openBrowser = async url => { const child = await require("open")(url, { wait: false }); child.unref(); },
   11 |   createClient = (id, secret, redirect) => new google.auth.OAuth2(id, secret, redirect) }) {
   12 |   const saved = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(credentialsPath, "utf8"));
   13 |   const keys = saved.installed;
   14 |   if (!keys?.client_id || !keys.client_secret) throw new Error("Booking authorization requires a Desktop OAuth credentials file; availability authorization was not changed");
   15 |   const state = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
   16 |   const verifier = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
   17 |   const challenge = createHash("sha256").update(verifier).digest("base64url");

EXPLANATION
Loads the supplied Desktop client credentials and creates a random state plus PKCE
challenge. These bind the browser result to the authorization attempt started by this PC
utility.

--- F21-B02: Local callback and authorization result ---
FILE: backend/lib/booking-oauth.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 18-49
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/lib/booking-oauth.js#L18-L49

CODE
   18 |   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   19 |     let client, redirect, timer, finished = false, exchanging = false;
   20 |     function finish(error, value) {
   21 |       if (finished) return;
   22 |       finished = true;
   23 |       clearTimeout(timer);
   24 |       server.close();
   25 |       if (error) server.closeAllConnections();
   26 |       if (error) reject(error); else resolve(value);
   27 |     }
   28 |     const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
   29 |       const url = new URL(req.url, "http://127.0.0.1");
   30 |       if (req.method !== "GET" || url.pathname !== "/oauth2callback") { res.writeHead(404).end("Not found"); return; }
   31 |       const received = Buffer.from(url.searchParams.get("state") || "");
   32 |       const expected = Buffer.from(state);
   33 |       if (received.length !== expected.length || !timingSafeEqual(received, expected)) { res.writeHead(400).end("Invalid authorization state"); return; }
   34 |       if (exchanging || finished) { res.writeHead(409).end("Authorization already being handled"); return; }
   35 |       if (url.searchParams.has("error") || !url.searchParams.get("code")) {
   36 |         res.writeHead(400).end("Authorization was not completed", () =>
   37 |           finish(new Error("Google authorization was declined; existing availability authorization was preserved")));
   38 |         return;
   39 |       }
   40 |       exchanging = true;
   41 |       try {
   42 |         const { tokens } = await client.getToken({ code: url.searchParams.get("code"), codeVerifier: verifier, redirect_uri: redirect });
   43 |         client.setCredentials(tokens);
   44 |         res.end("Booking authorization completed. Return to the terminal.", () => finish(null, client));
   45 |       } catch {
   46 |         res.writeHead(400).end("Authorization could not be completed", () =>
   47 |           finish(new Error("Google booking authorization failed; existing availability authorization was preserved")));
   48 |       }
   49 |     });

EXPLANATION
Runs a temporary loopback HTTP listener, validates the returned state, handles declined
consent and exchanges the code with its verifier. A timeout/error closes the attempt
without replacing the saved availability authorization.

--- F21-B03: Open the browser with the required permissions ---

CODE
   50 |     server.once("error", error => finish(error));
   51 |     timer = setTimeout(() => finish(new Error("Google booking authorization timed out; existing availability authorization was preserved")), timeoutMs);
   52 |     server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", async () => {
   53 |       try {
   54 |         redirect = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/oauth2callback`;

--- F22-B02: Save a granted authorization safely ---
FILE: backend/scripts/google-connect.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 11-34
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/scripts/google-connect.js#L11-L34

CODE
   11 | function persistAuthorization(client, credentialsPath, destination, requestedScopes = READ_SCOPES) {
   12 |   const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(credentialsPath, "utf8"));
   13 |   const keys = raw.installed || raw.web;
   14 |   if (!keys?.client_id || !keys.client_secret) throw new Error("The Google Desktop OAuth credentials file is incomplete");
   15 |   const granted = client.credentials.scope ? client.credentials.scope.split(/\s+/) : requestedScopes;
   16 |   if (!requestedScopes.every(scope => granted.includes(scope) || granted.includes("https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar"))) {
   17 |     throw new Error("Google did not grant all requested permissions; existing authorization was preserved");
   18 |   }
   19 |   let refreshToken = client.credentials.refresh_token;
   20 |   if (!refreshToken && fs.existsSync(destination)) {
   21 |     const previous = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(destination, "utf8"));
   22 |     if (previous.client_id === keys.client_id) refreshToken = previous.refresh_token;
   23 |   }
   24 |   if (!refreshToken) throw new Error("Google returned no reusable authorization. Revoke the old app grant in your Google account and authorize again");
   25 |   fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(destination), { recursive: true });
   26 |   const temporary = `${destination}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
   27 |   try {
   28 |     fs.writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify({ type: "authorized_user", client_id: keys.client_id,
   29 |       client_secret: keys.client_secret, refresh_token: refreshToken, nukmiris_scopes: granted }), { mode: 0o600, flag: "wx" });
   30 |     fs.renameSync(temporary, destination);
   31 |   } finally {
   32 |     if (fs.existsSync(temporary)) fs.unlinkSync(temporary);
   33 |   }
   34 | }

EXPLANATION
Checks granted permissions and a reusable refresh token. Writes to a temporary file with
restrictive permissions, then renames it. It keeps the availability and booking
destinations separate.

    9 | async function main() {
   10 |   loadLocalEnv();
   11 |   const filename = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", process.env.NUKMIRIS_DB_PATH || "data/nukmiris-sessions.sqlite");
   12 |   if (!fs.existsSync(filename)) throw new Error("Existing business database required; nothing was created");

   15 |     for (const table of ["businesses", "business_customers", "business_calendar_connections", "business_whatsapp_connections"]) {

   21 |     prepareBookingSchema(db);

   24 | }

--- F27-B02: Resolve a request only within the selected business ---
FILE: backend/scripts/reconcile-booking.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 9-26
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/scripts/reconcile-booking.js#L9-L26

CODE
    9 | async function main() {
   10 |   loadLocalEnv();
   11 |   const requestId = process.argv[2];
   12 |   if (!requestId || !/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/.test(requestId)) throw new Error("Supply the booking request ID");
   13 |   const filename = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", process.env.NUKMIRIS_DB_PATH || "data/nukmiris-sessions.sqlite");
   14 |   if (!fs.existsSync(filename)) throw new Error("Existing database required");
   15 |   const db = new DatabaseSync(filename);
   16 |   try {
   17 |     const businessId = process.env.NUKMIRIS_BUSINESS_ID || "juanita-nails";
   18 |     const row = db.prepare("SELECT sender_whatsapp_id FROM booking_requests WHERE request_id=? AND business_id=?").get(requestId, businessId);
   19 |     if (!row) throw new Error("Request does not belong to the selected business");
   20 |     const availability = createCalendarAvailability({ db, getClient: () => loadCalendarClient({ booking: true }) });
   21 |     const service = createBookingService({ db, availability, enabled: true });
   22 |     const result = await service.reconcile({ requestId, businessId, sender: row.sender_whatsapp_id });
   23 |     console.log(`Booking status: ${result.status}. This command never creates, changes, or deletes Google events.`);
   24 |     if (result.status === "uncertain") console.log("The calendar remains protected. Do not issue a new booking for this request; inspect the recorded event ID locally.");
   25 |   } finally { db.close(); }
   26 | }

EXPLANATION
Requires an existing request ID belonging to the selected business, gets its saved
sender and asks the service to reconcile. It reads Google and may update the local
verified outcome; it does not issue a fresh Calendar insertion, move or deletion.

--- F30-B02: Create or restore without replacing the live database ---
FILE: backend/scripts/database-backup.js | ORIGINAL LINES: 9-35

CODE
    9 | async function main() {
   10 |   loadLocalEnv();
   11 |   const directory = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", process.env.NUKMIRIS_BACKUP_DIR || ".local/backups");
   12 |   const action = process.argv[2];
   13 |   if (action === "--create") {
   14 |     const filename = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", process.env.NUKMIRIS_DB_PATH || "data/nukmiris-sessions.sqlite");
   15 |     if (!fs.existsSync(filename)) throw new Error("Existing database required");
   16 |     const db = new DatabaseSync(filename, { readOnly: true });
   17 |     try {
   18 |       const result = await createDatabaseBackup({ db, directory, keep: Number(process.env.NUKMIRIS_BACKUP_KEEP || 7) });
   19 |       console.log(`Verified database backup created: ${path.basename(result.filename)}`);
   20 |     } finally { db.close(); }
   21 |     return;
   22 |   }
   23 |   if (!["--restore-test", "--restore"].includes(action)) throw new Error("Choose a database backup command");
   24 |   const source = process.argv[3] && process.argv[3] !== "--to" ? path.resolve(process.argv[3]) : listBackups(directory)[0]?.filename;
   25 |   if (!source) throw new Error("Create a backup first");
   26 |   const position = process.argv.indexOf("--to");
   27 |   const destination = action === "--restore-test" ? path.join(directory, "restore-checks", `${randomUUID()}.sqlite`)
   28 |     : position >= 0 && process.argv[position + 1] ? path.resolve(process.argv[position + 1]) : null;
   29 |   if (!destination) throw new Error("Restore requires --to and a new unused destination filename");
   30 |   const live = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", process.env.NUKMIRIS_DB_PATH || "data/nukmiris-sessions.sqlite");
   31 |   if (path.resolve(destination) === live) throw new Error("The configured live database is never a restore destination");
   32 |   const result = await restoreDatabaseCopy({ source, destination });
   33 |   console.log(`Isolated restore verified: ${path.basename(result.filename)}. Integrity, foreign keys, and row counts match.`);
   34 |   console.log("The live database was not replaced. No app worker, Google calls, or WhatsApp sends were started.");
   35 | }

EXPLANATION
Create reads the configured database and produces a verified snapshot. Restore-test
chooses a separate temporary destination; restore requires a new destination. Both
reject using the configured live database as their output and report integrity/count
verification.

--- F31-B01: Imports and safe categories ---
FILE: backend/scripts/inspect-restart.cjs | ORIGINAL LINES: 1-42
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/scripts/inspect-restart.cjs#L1-L42

CODE
    1 | "use strict";
    2 | // Standalone, read-only diagnostic. Compatible with the published v0.4.0 DB.
    3 | // Run from backend/. No webhook, Calendar client, or delivery worker is loaded.
    4 | const fs = require("node:fs");
    5 | const path = require("node:path");
    6 | const { createHash } = require("node:crypto");
    7 | const { DatabaseSync } = require("node:sqlite");
    8 |
    9 | function parsed(value) { try { return JSON.parse(value); } catch { return null; } }
   10 | function utc(value) { const date = new Date(value); return Number.isFinite(date.getTime()) ? date.toISOString() : null; }
   11 | function reference(value) { return value ? createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex").slice(0, 12) : undefined; }
   12 | function action(message) {
   13 |   if (message?.type === "text") {
   14 |     const command = String(message.text?.body || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   15 |     return { kind: ["menu", "hola", "cita", "horarios", "reagendar", "cancelar"].includes(command) ? command : "private_text" };
   16 |   }
   17 |   const id = message?.interactive?.[message.interactive.type]?.id || "";
   18 |   const kind = /^menu:[^:]+:book$/.test(id) ? "main_menu_book"
   19 |     : /^menu:[^:]+:manage$/.test(id) ? "main_menu_manage"
   20 |     : /^availability:[^:]+:slot:/.test(id) ? "appointment_slot"
   21 |     : id.startsWith("availability:") ? "availability_navigation"
   22 |     : id.startsWith("booking:") ? "booking_status_or_decision"
   23 |     : id.startsWith("change:") ? "change_status_or_decision"
   24 |     : /^(manage|owned):/.test(id) ? "appointment_management" : "other_interactive";
   25 |   return { kind, buttonReference: reference(id) };
   26 | }
   27 | function replyKind(payload) {
   28 |   const text = payload?.text?.body || payload?.interactive?.body?.text || "";
   29 |   if (text === "Esta opción ya no está vigente. Escribe Cita para consultar horarios.") return "expired_main_menu";
   30 |   if (/ya no está vigente/.test(text)) return "expired_option";
   31 |   if (/está agendada/.test(text)) return "booking_success";
   32 |   if (/está reagendada/.test(text)) return "reschedule_success";
   33 |   if (/está cancelada/.test(text)) return "cancellation_success";
   34 |   if (/nombre completo/.test(text)) return "name_requested";
   35 |   if (/verificación/.test(text)) return "verification_pending";
   36 |   if (/No pude|No pudimos/.test(text)) return "request_error";
   37 |   if (payload?.interactive) return "interactive_choices";
   38 |   return "other_reply";
   39 | }
   40 | function safeError(value) {
   41 |   return /^(handler_failed|configuration_changed|reply_expired|restart_during_send|meta_delivery_failed|unreadable_meta_response|unverified_meta_response|meta_transport_unknown|meta_http_\d{3})$/.test(value || "") ? value : value ? "other_error" : null;
   42 | }

EXPLANATION
Loads SQLite without the webhook, worker or Calendar client. Classifies commands and
reply types, hashes button references and limits displayed error categories instead of
exporting private free text.

--- F31-B02: Read a consistent diagnostic report ---
FILE: backend/scripts/inspect-restart.cjs | ORIGINAL LINES: 43-83
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/scripts/inspect-restart.cjs#L43-L83

CODE
   43 | function inspectRestart({ db, businessId, limit = 50 }) {
   44 |   if (!businessId || !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 200) throw new Error("Select a business and a limit from 1 to 200");
   45 |   const exists = name => !!db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?").get(name);
   46 |   const result = { readOnly: true, messageTimes: "UTC receipt times; output records do not contain send timestamps",
   47 |     messageHistoryAvailable: exists("message_inbox") && exists("message_outbox"), messages: [], bookings: [] };
   48 |   const customers = new Map(), bookings = new Map();
   49 |   function alias(map, value, label) { if (!value) return null; if (!map.has(value)) map.set(value, `${label} ${map.size + 1}`); return map.get(value); }
   50 |   const customer = value => alias(customers, value, "Customer");
   51 |   const booking = value => alias(bookings, value, "Booking");
   52 |   db.exec("BEGIN");
   53 |   try {
   54 |     if (result.messageHistoryAvailable) {
   55 |       const records = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM message_inbox WHERE business_id=? ORDER BY inbox_id DESC LIMIT ?").all(businessId, limit).reverse();
   56 |       for (const row of records) {
   57 |         const snapshot = parsed(row.snapshot_json), session = parsed(snapshot?.session?.session_json);
   58 |         const continuation = parsed(row.continuation_json), incoming = action(parsed(row.message_json));
   59 |         const modes = ["main_menu", "availability", "booking_name", "booking_result", "booking_confirm", "management_menu", "management_select", "management_result", "reschedule_browse"];
   60 |         const replies = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM message_outbox WHERE inbox_id=? ORDER BY ordinal").all(row.inbox_id).map(reply => ({
   61 |           kind: replyKind(parsed(reply.payload_json)), state: reply.state, attempts: reply.attempts,
   62 |           metaStatus: ["sent", "delivered", "read", "failed"].includes(reply.meta_status) ? reply.meta_status : null,
   63 |           error: safeError(reply.last_error),
   64 |         }));
   65 |         result.messages.push({ inbox: row.inbox_id, customer: customer(row.sender), receivedAtUtc: utc(row.received_at),
   66 |           ...incoming, state: row.state, attempts: row.attempts, error: safeError(row.last_error),
   67 |           priorSession: modes.includes(session?.mode) ? session.mode : session ? "other_session" : null,
   68 |           priorOfferExpiredAtReceipt: Number.isFinite(session?.expiresAt) ? row.received_at > session.expiresAt : null,
   69 |           priorSelectionMatches: !!(session?.selectionCommand && reference(session.selectionCommand) === incoming.buttonReference),
   70 |           operation: continuation ? { kind: ["booking", "management"].includes(continuation.kind) ? continuation.kind : "other",
   71 |             booking: continuation.kind === "booking" ? booking(continuation.requestId) : null } : null, replies });
   72 |       }
   73 |     }
   74 |     if (exists("booking_requests")) {
   75 |       for (const row of db.prepare(`SELECT request_id,sender_whatsapp_id,state,created_at,updated_at FROM booking_requests
   76 |         WHERE business_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?`).all(businessId, limit)) {
   77 |         result.bookings.push({ booking: booking(row.request_id), customer: customer(row.sender_whatsapp_id),
   78 |           state: row.state, createdAtUtc: utc(row.created_at), updatedAtUtc: utc(row.updated_at) });
   79 |       }
   80 |     }
   81 |     return result;
   82 |   } finally { db.exec("ROLLBACK"); }
   83 | }

EXPLANATION
Reads recent message/booking records for one business inside a read transaction.
Replaces customer/booking identifiers with aliases and exposes limited timing, state and
recovery relationships. The transaction is rolled back; no business data is rewritten.

--- F31-B03: Standalone read-only command ---
FILE: backend/scripts/inspect-restart.cjs | ORIGINAL LINES: 84-103
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/backend/scripts/inspect-restart.cjs#L84-L103

CODE
   84 | function main() {
   85 |   const backend = process.cwd();
   86 |   require("dotenv").config({ path: path.join(backend, ".env"), quiet: true });
   87 |   const filename = path.resolve(backend, process.env.NUKMIRIS_DB_PATH || "data/nukmiris-sessions.sqlite");
   88 |   if (!fs.existsSync(filename)) throw new Error("Existing database required");
   89 |   const db = new DatabaseSync(filename, { readOnly: true });
   90 |   try {
   91 |     const report = inspectRestart({ db, businessId: process.env.NUKMIRIS_BUSINESS_ID || "juanita-nails" });
   92 |     const pkg = parsed(fs.readFileSync(path.join(backend, "package.json"), "utf8"));
   93 |     console.log(JSON.stringify({ tool: "NukMiris restart inspection", appVersion: /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(pkg?.version) ? pkg.version : "unknown",
   94 |       reliabilityEnabledNow: process.env.NUKMIRIS_ENABLE_RELIABILITY === "true", ...report }, null, 2));
   95 |   } finally { db.close(); }
   96 | }
   97 | if (require.main === module) {
   98 |   try { main(); } catch {
   99 |     console.error("Inspection stopped. Run from backend/ with Node 24 and the existing database/settings. No external services were contacted.");
  100 |     process.exitCode = 1;
  101 |   }
  102 | }
  103 | module.exports = { inspectRestart };

EXPLANATION
Reads the local database location, opens it read-only and prints the redacted report.
This utility can investigate old compatible reliability data without starting an app
worker or contacting Google/Meta.

--- F36-B02: Introduction and information categories ---
FILE: privacy.html | ORIGINAL LINES: 34-59
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/privacy.html#L34-L59

CODE
   34 | <body>
   35 | <main>
   36 |   <h1>NukMiris Privacy Policy</h1>
   37 |   <p><strong>Last updated: October 14, 2026</strong></p>
   38 |
   39 |   <p>
   40 |     NukMiris is an appointment management service currently
   41 |     in development. It is designed to help small businesses
   42 |     manage appointments and communicate with customers
   43 |     through WhatsApp.
   44 |   </p>
   45 |
   46 |   <h2>1. Information We May Collect</h2>
   47 |   <p>
   48 |     Depending on how NukMiris is used, information processed
   49 |     by the service may include:
   50 |   </p>
   51 |   <ul>
   52 |     <li>Customer names and telephone numbers.</li>
   53 |     <li>Appointment dates, times, and service details.</li>
   54 |     <li>WhatsApp messages related to appointment management.</li>
   55 |     <li>Appointment confirmations, cancellations, and changes.</li>
   56 |     <li>Business account and scheduling preferences.</li>
   57 |     <li>Technical information needed to operate and secure the service.</li>
   58 |   </ul>
   59 |

EXPLANATION
Explains the development-stage service and categories of information it may process.
These statements are website text, not database schema or executable permission checks.

--- F36-B03: Uses and provider integrations ---
FILE: privacy.html | ORIGINAL LINES: 60-92
VIEW ON GITHUB: https://github.com/vanasperena-ux/nukmiris/blob/2d69b6257064982e1b9b295186eb8ff12cfcbbd5/privacy.html#L60-L92

CODE
   60 |   <h2>2. How Information Is Used</h2>
   61 |   <p>
   62 |     Information may be processed to:
   63 |   </p>
   64 |   <ul>
   65 |     <li>Schedule and manage appointments.</li>
   66 |     <li>Send confirmations and appointment reminders.</li>
   67 |     <li>Process rescheduling and cancellation requests.</li>
   68 |     <li>Synchronize appointments with connected calendars.</li>
   69 |     <li>Maintain service reliability and security.</li>
   70 |     <li>Respond to support and privacy inquiries.</li>
   71 |   </ul>
   72 |
   73 |   <h2>3. WhatsApp Integration</h2>
   74 |   <p>
   75 |     NukMiris is designed to use the WhatsApp Business Platform,
   76 |     provided by Meta, for appointment-related communication.
   77 |     Relevant messages and contact details may be transmitted
   78 |     through Meta's services.
   79 |   </p>
   80 |
   81 |   <h2>4. Google Calendar Integration</h2>
   82 |   <p>
   83 |     Where a business authorizes Google Calendar integration,
   84 |     NukMiris may access calendar availability and create,
   85 |     update, or cancel appointment events as needed to provide
   86 |     scheduling functionality.
   87 |   </p>
   88 |   <p>
   89 |     Google Calendar information is intended to be used for
   90 |     appointment management and related service operations.
   91 |   </p>
   92 |

EXPLANATION
Describes intended uses plus WhatsApp and Google Calendar integration. The underlying
app capabilities remain determined by the actual server code and settings.

--- F36-B04: Providers, retention and deletion-request contact ---
FILE: privacy.html | ORIGINAL LINES: 93-131

   93 |   <h2>5. Service Providers and Data Sharing</h2>
   94 |   <p>
   95 |     NukMiris may rely on third-party technology providers,
   96 |     including Meta and Google, to deliver its functionality.
   97 |     Information may also be disclosed when required by
   98 |     applicable law or to protect the security of the service.
   99 |   </p>
  100 |   <p>
  101 |     NukMiris does not intend to sell customer personal
  102 |     information.
  103 |   </p>
  104 |
  105 |   <h2>6. Information Security and Retention</h2>
  106 |   <p>
  107 |     NukMiris aims to apply reasonable technical and
  108 |     organizational safeguards appropriate to the information
  109 |     it processes.
  110 |   </p>
  111 |   <p>
  112 |     Information will be retained according to operational
  113 |     requirements, applicable legal obligations, and
  114 |     documented retention practices as the service develops.
  115 |   </p>
  116 |
  117 |   <h2>7. Privacy Rights and Data Deletion</h2>
  118 |   <p>
  119 |     Individuals may contact NukMiris to request information
  120 |     about personal data associated with them, request
  121 |     corrections, or request deletion where applicable.
  122 |     Requests will be assessed under applicable law and
  123 |     relevant service obligations.
  124 |   </p>
  125 |   <p>
  126 |     Send privacy or deletion requests to:
  127 |     <a href="mailto:vanasperena@gmail.com">
  128 |       vanasperena@gmail.com
  129 |     </a>
  130 |   </p>
  131 |

EXPLANATION
Publishes policy statements and a contact link. Clicking the email link opens the user's
mail app; this page does not itself delete database records or implement a retention
job.

schema for those tests; it is not an automatic live migration at startup.

OTHER REPOSITORY MATERIAL
--------------------------
README and backend/*.md: setup, release scope, evidence and limitations.
docs/ROADMAP.md: saved project continuity and Windows npm.cmd preference.
backend/package-lock.json: generated exact dependency versions/integrity data.
docs/handoffs/: historical handoff/archive, not the current runtime entry point.
node_modules/: installed external dependencies, not NukMiris-authored business logic.
Actual .env, credentials, token caches, SQLite data and backups: local operational
data, excluded from this walkthrough. No current customer data was needed.

SOURCE ACCURACY CHECK
----------------------
This reading copy covers 36 complete application/configuration/utility/
website files: 4726 original source lines in 225 explained blocks.
Every reproduced numbered line was matched to the pinned Git source, and every
covered file has contiguous ranges from line 1 through its original final line.
The application version remains 0.7.3; creating this guide is not a new release.
